# ANSI_TODO — render.dll 作为第三方类库的补充路线

本页只剩**一张台账**（§3）。工单、判决、证人、状态都在同一行的同一格里，不再需要跨页对照。
姊妹文档：`ANSI_SUPPORTS.md`（现状支持矩阵）、`CONEMU_ANSI_DEFECTS.md`（上游缺陷清单）、
`MSFT_TERMINAL_REFERENCE.md`（WT 参照）、`DESIGN.md`（设计与 I-规则）、`JLINE_CHANGES.md`（动过依赖的登记）、`TERMINFO.md`（caps）。

## 0 本页规则

**新旧章节对照**（旧引用一律按此重定向）：旧 §1 → §1；旧 §2–§6、§7（不做清单的**重排前原文表**）、§8–§11、T5 各"腿"节 → **附录 A**（逐字）与 §6/§7；
旧 §7 的现行判决 → §5（2026-09-28 逐行复核过，删了一行已落地的、改了四条理由、补了五条散在表里的）；
旧 §10 与旧 §12 的两张表 → **§3 一张表（一行一个编号）**；旧 §12 表后那段
"the honest gaps" → §7；旧 §9「建议顺序」已作废（那些条目全部落了，看 §3 的 `状态`/`build`）。

**立页原文（2026-09-25 快照，照录不改写）**：

快照 2026-09-25。上游参照：**jline4**（`D:\JavaProjects\jline4`，这是dbcli将会更新的第三方库，render.dll 的真实调用方）+ **Windows Terminal**（本地克隆 `cache/msfterm`，行号坐标沿用 `MSFT_TERMINAL_REFERENCE.md` 的已核清单）+ **ghostty**。
姊妹文档：`ANSI_SUPPORTS.md`（现状支持矩阵）、`CONEMU_ANSI_DEFECTS.md`（上游缺陷清单）、`MSFT_TERMINAL_REFERENCE.md`（WT 参照）。

**定调**：补充清单不从"新终端有什么"出发，从**jline4 实际打到 render.dll 的字节**出发——caps 驱动的路径（I26）已经全部建模，剩下的差距全在 jline4 的直发字面量里。下表是逐树 grep 过的主源码清单（测试与 demo 排除）。

---

- **旧引用等价**：`DESIGN.md §10`、"DESIGN.md 的 #NN 行"、本页旧 §10 与旧 §12，自 2026-09-28 起重定向到本页 **§3 的 #NN 行**。
  `DESIGN.md` 那一节留指针。源码注释里的旧指针（`.cpp`/`.java` 里若干）未改写，属主自行决定。
- **2026-09-28 这次重排做了什么**：旧 §10（29 行，`# | 项 | 旧理由 | 新判定 | 可行性/代价/门禁`）与旧 §12（95 行，
  `# | Subject | Where it lives | Proof, and what it printed`）合并成一张表，**按编号升序**；18 个在两表里都有的编号
  合成一行，两栏原文都保留；夹在表格行之间的段落折回它所属的那一格；单元格内未转义的 `|` 一律改成 `\|`
  （此前有 7 行因此在前端渲染成断表）。**判决文字一律照抄，没有改写、没有删减、没有去重。**
- **改写前原文**：`cache/ansi-todo-rewrite/ANSI_TODO.md.20260928-0000`（md5 `3aeaeda0639423fea79f8bd72f8bfa1e`，
  770 行 / 301,121 B）。旧 §1–§11 与「T5 腿 1-6 / 窗口腿 / 执行指针」的正文被本节以下的内容取代，原文都在那份备份里；
  要回滚就整份覆盖回去。
- **`状态`/`档位`/`build` 三列是关键词抽取，不是判定**：它们由同一行的原文归纳，只用于排序与筛选；
  与原文冲突时**一律以 `判决与证人` / `工单` 两列的文字为准**。抽取不到就写 `未标`，不猜。
- **复核这张表的顺序**（这是本页存在的主要用途：将来对代码与测试做复核）：
  ① 先读 §6 的开放清单；② 再挑 `档位=none` 或 `状态=未标` 的行——这些是"结论没有腿或腿写不清"的一类；
  ③ 对每一行按 `证人文件` 列里的路径去 `stat`/重跑，路径不存在或日志早于当前 build 号 ⇒ 该行的证人**已过期**，
  需要重跑而不是引用；④ `位置` 列里的 `file:line` 在被引用前必须自己打开确认（本台账历史上有多处行号腐烂）；
  ⑤ 两列原文里出现的"撤回/改判/作废/推翻/stale"字样都是**有效结论**，不是噪音——它们防止后人重做已被否证的方案。
- **编号空洞是故意的**：缺号 = 预留位或已并入他行，`1041` 不是工单号而是对 #85 在 -41..-44 出货内容的更正。
  `T1..T6` 是 `WORK_ORDER.md` 自己的编号：**T1=#49（-16）、T2=#59（-18）、T3=#50（-17）、T4=#51（-18）、T5=#58（-17）**，
  **T6 从未作为任务建过**（工单标记可选，至今开着，理由见 #95 与 §6）。

## 1 jline4 真实发射清单（消费契约）

定调：补充清单不从"新终端有什么"出发，从 **jline4 实际打到 render.dll 的字节** 出发——caps 驱动的路径（I26）已全部建模，

**Windows Terminal**（本地克隆 `cache/msfterm`）。

| 序列 | 发射点 | render.dll 现状 |
|---|---|---|
| **探测批**：`CSI ?u` + `CSI ?2026$p` + `CSI ?2027$p` + `CSI ?2048$p` + `CSI c` | `AbstractTerminal.probeModes()`（AbstractTerminal.java:620-631，`CSI_DEC="\033[?"`:113）；任何 `isModeSupported()` 首调触发，reader 开 `Option.KITTY_KEYBOARD` 即走（LineReaderImpl.java:703→704） | `?u` **误执行成 DECRC（P0）**；`$p` 忽略+计数；`c` 有应答 |
| `CSI ?2026h/l`（BSU/ESU） | `Display` 全屏更新（Display.java:134-135/495/846，byte 模式 `rawEsc`）+ `Terminal.begin/endSynchronizedUpdate`（Terminal.java:1505/1529） | 计数 `RC_UN_MODE` 丢弃 ⇒ 同步输出未生效（P1） |
| `CSI ?2004h/l` | LineReaderImpl.java:707-711（`Option.BRACKETED_PASTE`） | 计数 `RC_UN_DECBP`；输入侧，忽略正确 |
| `CSI 9999E` | LineReaderImpl.java:6623（ConEmu activate hack，仅 `TYPE_WINDOWS_CONEMU`） | 已支持（`E` 臂钳制）✓ |
| `CSI ?1004h/l`、鼠标 `?1000…` | Windows 腿 `trackFocus/trackMouse` 覆写为**只置内存标志**（AbstractWindowsTerminal.java:501/717），不发字节 | 不在电线上 ✓ |
| caps 驱动（csr/sc/rc/cup/el/ed/il/dl/ich/dch/ech/indn/rin/1049/u6/u7…） | `Status.java`、`Display.java`、reader | 全部已建模（I26）✓ |
| OSC（title/hyperlink/133…） | jline4 主干 **0 处直发**；133 是 dbcli 自己发的（I23 已建模） | — |
| 图形（Sixel/iTerm2/Kitty） | `SixelGraphics` 等存在，由探测结果门控（见 §4 的 SIXEL 误报） | DCS 载荷丢弃+`RC_UN_DCS` 计数 |

Re-witnessed at -13 with one identical 170-char line typed into two geometries (`cache/witness/wide1516-20260925-13.txt`, **`#16 VERDICT: ok for the design claim`**): with a 2000-column buffer and a 125-column window it lands as a single row `4x170` at columns 5..174, `r05` empty, no warning block -- the line is as wide as the buffer, and `cols-used=125` says only that far of it is *viewed*. With the window made the buffer (`-Mode 'cols=125 lines=30'`) the same line **folds rather than trims**: `4x120` on `r07` plus 50 characters continuing on `r08`, cursor `(50,8)`, all 170 present -- so Lua's warning text ("default to be trimmed") describes a path this build does not take; the trimming branch was left unhunted by instruction, and the finding is recorded as a finding. Two things this row must not be read as claiming: the cursor at `124,4` in leg A is not a defect but `Paint.cpp:201-224`, which clamps the *parked* column into the window because conhost slides the viewport sideways to include a cursor it is told about (measured 2026-09-23, same comment), while the model keeps the true column; and this A/B is of line width, not of a scroll -- §9's wide-buffer-scroll leg is still owed. |  |

| 47 | 未标 | host+live |  | `cache/status47-48-20260925/` | Fix the status bar's separator rule not following a resize -- closed: the rule is geometry, handed back to the `Status.setBorder` template | `terminal/.../utils/Status.java:127-151` (`DEFAULT_BORDER`, `setBorder(AttributedString,int)`, `getBorderString(columns)` tiling to `columns + overflow`, cache keyed on `columnLength()` not `length()`) + `Console.setStatus` (template call, `titles` reduced to one row, `prevWidth` and its guard deleted) | Third root cause; the first two are recorded and voided ("Windows never raises WINCH", and "the flush fast path reused `prevWidth`", which was a real but secondary half). The actual fault: **the separator is geometry and dbcli was minting it as text** -- `Console.setStatus` built a fresh run of `-` at `getScreenWidth()-1` and pushed it into `titles`, `resize()` dutifully re-sent the stored content, and `update()` pads any short line with spaces, so a 99-dash rule on a 145-column screen *looked* full-width to every layer downstream. Nothing below that point could repair it, and `LineReaderImpl.handleSignal` was already calling `status.resize()` while parked -- it was only drawing stale content more often. Hence the fix is in the library, tiling a template per draw, same shape as the library's own `getBorderString`. Live witness, classic console leg (`--host auto`, idle frames), `cache/status47-48-20260925/{leg47c.json,bar-rule.py}`: red `h-after-00/01 cols=145 rows=34 rule at row 33 dashes=99 want=144 STALE` -- and `h-help-00` reaching `144 match` only *after* a statement, which is exactly "a new setStatus rescues it" -- against green `i-after-00/01/02 dashes=144 want=144 match` with one rule row on screen (32 rule / 33 status text / 31 prompt), no double, no residue. I32 is the contract; what belongs in a ledger is how it was found to be broken. **The gate caught a real defect on the way**: the region's *first* held flush only started the clock and then fell through and painted -- so the frame an application writes together with its BSU (which is the normal shape: BSU plus the top of the new screen) went up over the old frame, and the flicker this task exists to kill survived precisely the one chunk where it matters most. | 57 | ✅ 已落地 | host+live+window | -24 | `/cache/p63/deployed` `cache/p57/live7.txt` `cache/p57/live8.txt` `cache/p63/dbcli.jar.20260926-025811.bak` | ANSI_TODO P2: the OSC 52 clipboard (off by default + an explicit switch + a whitelist) | `Render.h` (`RC_OSC_MAX`, `RC_TITLE_MAX`, `RC_CLIP_*`, `RC_UN_OSC_CLIP`, the grid's `clip[]/nClip/clipPending` and the four `nClip*` tallies), `Render.cpp` (`rc_b64_decode`, `osc_clip`, `g_clipPolicy`, and the `rc_osc_families` table that dispatches to them), `RenderJni.cpp` (`clip_apply`, the pending block in `paint_flush`, `STAT_CLIP`'s seven slots, `setClipboardPolicy0`/`clipboardPolicy0`), `NativeRenderer.java` (`ANSI_CLIPBOARD`, the two wrappers, the report clause), `RenderCheck.cpp` (`gm_clipboard`, `geo_osc_families`), `Render.java` (`caseClipboard`), `build.sh` (29 exports and the derived `gate_declared`) | Landed as build -24. **The row's title is still the design**: the sequence is one escape away from copying anything a program prints into the user's clipboard, so the default is off and off is what a session gets without having asked. Only the host can turn it on -- `ANSI_CLIPBOARD=on\|1\|true\|yes` read at class-init, or `NativeRenderer.setClipboardPolicy` -- and nothing in the byte stream can reach either, which is what makes "off" mean off rather than "not yet asked". There is no ASK value, because this library owns no window to ask in; a host that wants a prompt implements it around the same call. Four refusals, each with its own counter so a report can say which happened: a selection field other than `c` or empty (Windows has one clipboard, so `p`/`s`/`q`/`a`/`b`/`c`/`d`/`0-7` are not folded -- ghostty folds only because X11 and macOS really do have those registers, `stream_terminal.zig:678-682`); a **read** (`52;c;?`), refused with the write switch on too, because the reply would put what the user last copied into the console's *input* stream (ghostty answers it straight to the pty, `:820-826`, from a terminal that also has a `clipboard-read=.ask` policy to gate it -- here the answer is a refusal with no policy to configure); a payload that fails a strict RFC 4648 decode; and one over the cap. Strict means: whole-payload or nothing, never a partial decode; interior whitespace refused rather than skipped (a multi-line wrap is not one message); the unused bits of a final group required to be zero (§3.5); a decoded NUL refused whole, because `CF_UNICODETEXT` is NUL-terminated and storing the prefix hands the user half a paste; and UTF-8 validated with `MB_ERR_INVALID_CHARS` before the UTF-16 conversion, a deliberate split from ghostty's unvalidated bytes. An empty payload is the *clear* request, not an absent one. Nothing is restored at `close()`: snapshotting the register would be the read this half refuses. **ConEmu has no OSC 52 at all** -- measured, not assumed: its OSC switch is `switch (*Code.ArgSZ)` with cases 0/1/2/4/9… and no `case L'5'` -- so ConEmu gives this row no parity to copy -- **but MSFT and ghostty both implement it and both default to allowing it** (`compatibility.allowOSC52` = true in `ControlProperties.h:59`; `clipboard-write = .allow` in ghostty `Config.zig:2459`), which is written down here precisely because it means the off-by-default is a divergence this project has to justify on its own merits, and I36 does: a terminal the user configures is a consenting party, a renderer inside someone else's JVM is not. The one place the references agree is honoured -- neither answers a read (WT `OutputStateMachineEngine.cpp:825`, ghostty `clipboard-read=.ask`) -- and one split is deliberate: WT ignores the selection field (":1097", "Currently the first parameter `Pc` is ignored") where this build refuses it. Four bugs, and the gates caught all four. **One: the decoder's own first bug** -- the non-canonical-tail mask read the bottom of the accumulator instead of the bottom of the *group*, so `QR==` (a legal-looking 'A' with a hidden bit set) decoded instead of being refused; caught by the host assertion written for exactly that case. **Two: a mis-bound JNI export**, `Java_..._setClipboardPolicy` written for a Java method named `setClipboardPolicy0` -- invisible to a whitelist copied by hand from the same wrong assumption, and invisible at run time *because the wrapper catches Throwable so an older dll keeps working*, which is also how it reads forever: "policy off". `build.sh` now derives the required names from the `native` declarations themselves (`gate_declared`: 12 in `Render.java`, 18 in `NativeRenderer.java`) so the list cannot be wrong in that direction again. **Three: the gate's reader was the writer's cache** -- AWT, once this JVM has called `setContents`, answers from the object it was handed, so every readback returned the gate's own string whatever the library wrote: counters said one write, the readback said the text never changed, and the two were describing different clipboards. Eight red assertions looked like a broken painter. `caseClipboard` now witnesses the clipboard from a second process (`pwsh Set-/Get-Clipboard`), and skips loudly where that is unavailable rather than falling back to the reader that can lie. **Four:** `OSC 52;;` never cleared anything, because `MultiByteToWideChar` returns 0 for an empty input and the painter read that as a failed conversion -- and because the Win32 sequence has no `EmptyClipboard()` in it, which is both the documented order and the only way a clear can be expressed. Gates: host `checks=4065 fails=0` (3810 at -23; `gm_clipboard`'s ~48 assertions and `geo_osc_families`' 142 are the difference -- the family table is now provably disjoint, exactly as wide as its probes, and no handler is silent), live `checks=5517 failures=0` on both architectures and again against copies of the installed bytes (`run.ps1 -Scratch D:/dbcli/cache/p63/deployed`). Deployed: `lib/x86` -> `8f6e57baec97ef4569bede145f23f78e` (149087 -> 156705) and `lib/x64` -> `604e05c787ed38739f00d8c03cc557bb` (144958 -> 152491), backups `render.dll.20260926-025516.bak`, and `run.ps1 -Scratch D:/dbcli/cache/p63/deployed` re-ran against copies of the installed bytes **twice** (`cache/p57/live7.txt`, `cache/p57/live8.txt`): the first -24 link hashed `1f78169b…`/`729191ce…` at these same sizes, and a later comment-only re-link moved both hashes without moving a byte of code -- §8's rule that md5 is file identity, not program identity, paid for itself again. `lib/dbcli.jar` -> `fe2a4ed01bb7d13c61dbbb202059fd58` with exactly **one** entry differing (`com/hyee/ansirender/NativeRenderer.class`, `f57364c9…` -> `18c1da7c…`) out of 132, proven entry-by-entry rather than by the jar's own hash; backup `cache/p63/dbcli.jar.20260926-025811.bak`. Arms 1, 2 and 4 were each demonstrated red with one deliberate edit and no other leg moved; **arm 3 was not** -- the only way found to make the dump differ is the way that also makes `tic` complain, which leg 1 catches, so leg 3 stays as the guard against a future ncurses folding or renaming a capability, and that limit is written here rather than in a comment. `TERMINFO.md` also answers the question the jline4 entry raises: of the eight capabilities upstream declares and this one does not, only `kmous` (`MouseSupport.java:85`) and `cbt` (`LineReaderImpl.java:6992`) have readers -- the first is correctly absent because the mouse modes are counted and not implemented, the second is a real (small) cost whose fix is #70, and the remaining six have no reader anywhere in jline4 —— **#112 把那"六条"点名了，并且把理由逐条归位**：拿 jline master 的原始条目（`cache/jline-upstream-windows-conemu.caps`，1090 B，2026-09-28 经代理取回）与本仓镜像做集合差（脚本与结果 `cache/p112-setdiff.txt`）⇒ upstream 声明 80 项、我们 91 项，**我们缺的正好七项**：`blink, invis, kmous, mc5i, mir, ncv, rmpch`；去掉确有读者的 `kmous` 就是那句说的六条。而"没有读者"按属主 09-28 第二条裁决不是理由，所以六条各自落到能站住的那一类：`blink`/`invis` = **① 没有承载位**（#94 逐字读过 `conattrs.hpp:9` 的 `META_ATTRS` 全集，只有 lead/trail、三条 grid line、reverse、underscore）；`mc5i` = **①** 本库没有任何打印/透传面；`ncv` = **①** 没有"不可共存"位可表达；`rmpch` = **①** 没有字体切换；`mir` = **② 对另一个解析器撒谎**（CEAnsi 把 `?4` 记为忽略，与 `smir`/`rmir` 同判）；`kmous` = **③** 它会宣传本库不写的报告（#95/#108 那道设计题）。**顺带修本行自己的腐烂**：句中说 `cbt` 属于"upstream 有、我们没有"的八项 —— #90 之后 `cbt` 已在条目内，本轮差集也证明它不在缺项里，故那个"八"应为"七"；本行括号里"seven remain"的更正是对的一半，前半句没跟着改. *(#90 moved every number in this row: 91 capabilities, md5 `1f3e082c3f12`, and `cbt` is declared, so seven remain -- and it also corrected this row's own citation, since `LineReaderImpl.java:6992` is jline4's line and this tree's is `:6420`.)* |  |

`caseMouseModes`；三臂突变分别红 `fails=24/9/2`）。**本工单另外两件没做**：`SetConsoleMode(ENABLE_MOUSE_INPUT)` 的借还与 close 归还原样，以及由此才谈得上的 `kmous` ⇒ 拆给 #95。`kmous` 本轮**继续不声明**，理由不是"还没轮到"而是**声明即撒谎**：条目里 `kmous` 是在邀请应用发 `?1000h` 并承诺事件会到，而本库一个鼠标事件都不生成（`caseMouseModes` 的注释把这条写成了显式的"本腿不证、也永不证"）。同轮还带出一条真缺陷：#81 使"一次写里问全一个族"成为合法载荷（9 > `RC_REPORT_MAX` 的 8）⇒ 第九个应答被丢弃，上限改 64，见 #95 上一段与 §11 第 5 条 |
| 82 | ◐ 已落地有余项 | host+live | -37 | `cache/audit90/host-37.txt` `cache/audit90/b37-live37.txt` `cache/audit90/b37-live36red.txt` `cache/audit90/render` | `?2048` in-band size report: store the bit, answer, cleared by both resets, carried across a rebuild, **and actually get the report sent** | `Render.h:511` (`inbandResize`, immediately after `bracketedPaste`), `Render.h:865-871` (the contract of `rc_notify_geometry`: returning 1/0 is the caller's business, and a report a full queue refused is retried next time rather than written off), `Render.cpp:1899-1906` (`?2048 h/l` stores the bit, off by default), `:1536` (`mode_status`'s `case 2048`), `:1419` (cleared by RIS) / `:1460` (cleared by DECSTR), `:3415` (`rc_reset_hist` initialisation), `:3174-3180` (the body of `rc_notify_geometry`), `:1519` (the old silence ruling rewritten as a historical note, not deleted), `RenderJni.cpp:385`+`:1315` (the handle's `repRows`/`repCols` = "what the stream was last told"), `:1733-1735` (seeded inside `open`, so the first report has to be a **change** rather than a greeting), `:781-785` (**landing point: the head of the `flush_reports` queue**), `:1394`/`:1442` (carried across a rebuild), `:709-718` (spelling out `\x1b[8;<winRows>;<cols>t`), `RenderCheck.cpp:1208-1232` (the family of legs that rewrote the old ruling legs), `geo_inband` `:3226-3290`, `geo_decrpm_silence` `:3194` (`SILENT[]` 11 entries → 10, 2048 moved out) and `:3210-3214` (the must-answer set 5 → 6 votes), `Render.java:1946 caseInbandResize` (mounted at `:199`) | Landed 2026-09-27 as build **-37**. **Two re-rulings, both better than the original plan in `ANSI_TODO.md` §11:** (1) **do not send `CSI 48;…t`; reuse the `ESC [ 8 ; winRows ; cols t` that `18t` already sends**. All three oracles checked: only ghostty has this family -- `src/terminal/modes.zig:381` names 2048 `in_band_size_reports`, `src/terminal/size_report.zig:45-55` encodes `CSI 48;rows;cols;heightPx;widthPx t`, and `csi_18_t` at `:73-78` of the same file is exactly `ESC [ 8 ; rows ; cols t`; the local msfterm clone gives **0 hits** for `2048` across `src/terminal/`+`src/host/` (so §11's sentence "MSFT's shape is `CSI 48;height;width t`" is **without a source**, and it is voided once ghostty's own text was re-read this round), and wezterm gives 0 hits too => this is a sequence only one of the three implements, so whichever side you take, you have to say so explicitly. We **deliberately deviate from ghostty's spelling**: I42 says a geometry may have only one name -- the same two numbers in the same order are already answered once by `18t`, so minting another report would be two spellings of one fact (the reader has to learn it twice, `reply_text` gains a branch, and every existing assertion in `geo_census`/`caseWindowOps` moves house); the harder reason is that the last two numbers of ghostty's report are **pixels**, which needs `cell_size_px`, and this tree has no pixel authority -- `#79` keeps `14t`/`16t` silent for exactly that reason, and putting two guessed numbers out of four into a shipping report is I26. **The second deliberate deviation from ghostty**: ghostty replies with the current geometry the instant `?2048` **is switched on** (`src/terminal/stream_terminal.zig:1664-1667` -> `reportMode2048` `:1499-1508`); we keep quiet, and the leg `nothing for the shape the session is already in` pins that -- the byte would be input the program never wrote, the same family as `u6`/`u7` and OSC 52's "off by default" (I26/I36).(2) **The landing point is in flush, not in `rc_reset_hist` and not in `build_model`.** Rejected by measurement: `rc_reset_hist` zeroes `reportHead/reportLen` while building the new grid, so anything `arm_report`ed inside it is wiped on the spot (four legs read "empty queue + an uninitialised `RcReportItem`", with `it.kind` coming back as 0x42 and `it.mode` as an ASCII fragment such as 0x380033; a rule falls out of that in passing -- while the queue is empty `rc_report_take` does not touch the out-param, so a leg has to assert `rc_report_pending(&g)==1` before it takes, otherwise what it reads is stack garbage and not "no report"). And `build_model` runs `rc_reset_hist` on a **fresh** grid, with the bits restored from the old grid's `keep*` => the judgement "the geometry changed + the mode is on" can only happen **after** the restore. Sitting at the head of `flush_reports` brings two free benefits besides: what gets compared is "the shape the stream was last **told** about", so the sources the JNI side cannot see -- "the user dragged the window bigger", "another program ran `mode con:`" -- travel the same path; and the host gate never links `reply_text` at all => **#82's witness can only be a live leg** (`readopt`, the same shape as the carry legs of #77/#79/#80/#83). **Two old legs that had to be rewritten rather than added to** (the same lesson as #78/#83): `reflow and in-band resize answer nothing` narrowed to 2027 only, and `each votes for the mode it asked about` split into a paired assertion -- "2048 has both doors open, 2027 has neither" -- while 2048 moved out of `geo_decrpm_silence`'s `SILENT[]`; leaving it there would be two tables each saying the same thing about the same number.Proof: host `checks=6355 fails=0` + `RENDERCHECK: ok` (`cache/audit90/host-37.txt`; 6287→6355). Live, **run against copies of the deployed `lib/` bytes, with `-Install`**: x86 and x64 each `checks=6094 failures=0`, `STAGE0 RUN: ok`, `SKIP` count **0** (`cache/audit90/b37-live37.txt`). **The red that was seen on the spot**: the same `Render.java` pointed at the -36 shipping bytes => `checks=6094 failures=8` on each architecture, `STAGE0 RUN: failed` (`cache/audit90/b37-live36red.txt`), 6 of them being `caseInbandResize`'s own (`FAIL 2, because the bit is stored  got= want=\e[?2048;2$y`, `FAIL arrives with one unsolicited report, spelled like 18t and carrying the new viewport  got= want=\e[8;25;200t`, `FAIL it is counted as a reply written, not as a refusal`, `FAIL the mode is still the application's`, `FAIL 1, not set`, `FAIL it costs no refused-mode vote`), and the other 2 being `caseReports`' jline4 probe batch. The eight mutation arms: M3 does not answer => 13 host reds; M2 reports `rows` instead of `winRows` => 1 host leg (`mode carries the viewport's rows: got 0x18 want 0x6`); M4 RIS does not clear => 1; M8 does not store the bit => 1; whereas **M1 does not queue the report / M5 does not carry it / M6 does not gate the mode / M7 reports on every flush -- all four of these arms are green on the host gate (6355/0) and only the live leg sees them** (`failures=2 / 3 / 22 / 25`) -- the first quantification of the fact that, among a mode bit's five obligations, the answer and the action **do not live in the same gate**, which is the entire reason §11 makes #82's witness a live leg. The ticket's own words were "纯解析 + 一张静态表 + 生成脚本" (parsing only + one static table + a generator script), and **that sentence underestimates two things**.The first is the table's identity: this is not "copy a table from a reference" but four local sources (ghostty's `src/terminal/res/rgb.txt`, wezterm's `color-types/src/rgb.txt`, the 2020 OS package copy, and msfterm's own `colorTable.cpp` re-derived) crossed by **two parsers** (ghostty's fixed-column slice `x11_color.zig:45-48` and wezterm's whitespace split `color-types/src/lib.rs:393-403`), where all four cells must yield the same set -- the two vendored copies **differ byte for byte** (B is CRLF and 97 lines rearrange the numeric columns with a TAB), which is exactly what makes "they agree" informative; and what is really being guarded against is "some vendor refresh pushes a digit past column 11 => a component off by a factor of 16 while the line count stays the same", which no check that reads one file with one parser can see. The second is that **two existing legs had "names fail" as their own witness**: what `a name is counted` pinned was precisely `orange` being refused -- QUEUE.md had warned beforehand that "orange 是真 X11 名，这条会意外通过" (orange is a real X11 name, so this leg will pass by accident), and **it had the direction backwards**: the leg did not pass, it went **red** (the very first build reported `names fail on the way: got 0x0 want 0x2`), because once names take effect nobody votes any more; the second place is nastier -- the `]10;red;green` leg used to prove MSFT's one-field-per-resource walk-forward with "two refusals", and once names resolve the count falls to zero, so **if you merely re-baseline the assertion to 0, the walk-forward ruling loses its witness a second time**. The rewritten shape guesses no numbers: send `red` alone and record the foreground nibble, send `green` alone and record the background nibble (pinning that it equals the 10 that `rgb:00/ff/00` gives), then send the two together and require each nibble to equal its own -- "walking forward" is still proven, only no longer by way of a failure count. Proof: host `checks=6518 fails=0` (6434→6518 = +84). **Three mutation arms, each attributable** (lab `cache/audit90/t2mut/`, `t2_mut.py`, driver `t2build.sh <arm>`): delete the name arm => `6518/16`, all 16 named individually (among them `and lands on the very bytes the sharp form gives`, `a prefix of a name is not the name`, `a sharp form is never a name`, and the two walk-forward legs speaking up again with "count 0x2" when the names are gone); make an unknown name lie about success => `6518/8`, and **one of those is an existing leg**, `other OSC counted: got 0x1 want 0x2` -- census drift has sentinels elsewhere too, which is precisely why "a refusal must be counted" deserves a leg of its own; drop the case-insensitive fold => `6518/10`, with the two spelling legs **exchanging exactly** (camel reports `want 0xFF0000 got 0x9AFA00`, spaced the other way round) -- a fingerprint only a mis-fold produces. Removing the arms returns to green at `6518/0`, and `t2_mut.py restore` resets `Render.cpp` and the vendor header together. Both are the same class of mistake (treating the model's table as the screen's table), so those two legs are now "compare against the `rgb:` spelling" and "compare against what was there before the write" -- both guess-free assertions; (2) a QUEUE prediction can point the wrong way -- "will pass by accident" was in fact "will go red", and every judgement of the shape "some existing leg will pass silently" has to be run once yourself before it is believed; (3) a generated artifact that goes into `vendor/` needs a `--check`, and that `--check` **only counts once it has itself been caught red-handed**: change one byte -> `stale_rc=1` -> put it back -> `0` (observed live this round, roughly 100 s per run under Git Bash; under WSL the `/d/` path does not exist => it reports rc=2 honestly and only warns, instead of faking a failure). Four arms (lab `cache/audit90/t3mut/`): `g1` **6638/5**, `so` **6638/2**, the first version of the `sgr0clear` leg **6638/0 (a false green)** → after the change **6672/2**, and `carry` **completely green on the host, 6672/0** ⇒ its witness can only be in the live run; the first version of the live probe was also **6142/0 green**, and only after it was changed to "G0 ordinary / G1 line-drawing" did it produce **6142/1×2**. ⚠ **Both false greens share one cause**: the probe loaded G0 and G1 with the same line-drawing set, ⇒ "drop the shift and fall back to G0" still drew the same box; the criterion is "**when you ask whether the shift is alive, nothing else in the probe may be able to produce the same glyph**".Three more traps this row earned: (1) `CSI J` is erase **down** -- sending it from home sweeps the buffer, `winT` goes wild and the whole leg reads back empty (7 rows of `= U+20`); (2) `standardGeometry()` only re-opens the **model** cursor, while the **console** cursor is left on the column the previous case wrote ⇒ every probe must carry its own CUP, and after a `readopt` you need `winT = consoleView()[0]`; (3) **the anchor may not be a letter in 0x60..0x7E** -- in the DEC line-drawing set `r` *is* U+208B (subscript zero), so its being remapped is precisely what the leg sets out to prove, yet it reads like a failed re-address; switched to `#`. The price of the new census slot is recorded here too: once `RC_UN_MAX` changes, **every family after titles shifts by +1 in the stats array**, and the two Java tables are hardcoded literals ⇒ the first live run was **55 reds with a one-sentence root cause** (`stats() ... length=58 against a table of 57`), and `geo_census`'s `the labels, in this order, unchanged` bit me in the very commit that added the row -- **it was the only thing this round that stopped "change just one place"**. Two existing legs were rewritten (`an ESC ) designator has no effect` and `the CSI p family and ESC ) c are counted == 3`): both had been using "G1 is unreachable" as their witness, and the fix was to replace them with assertions that really distinguish old from new in their semantics + a positive assertion on the `charset` slot, not to delete assertions.The ticket's sentence "`ENQ`/`ESC . c` 仍计数" ("`ENQ`/`ESC . c` are still counted") is **false in both halves**: `ESC .` is an intermediate byte and `c` goes through `esc_dispatch`'s `case 'c'` = RIS (a reset, not a count); `ENQ` lands in `control()`'s `default:` (neither counted nor painted) -- the legs are pinned to the real behaviour, and the comment spells out why copying the ticket would have erected a false leg. // Reset all character set designations.`。链条这次逐行开过：`SoftReset`(:2984) → `terminalOutput.cpp:34-40` → `RestoreFrom` 的 `*this = savedState`(:49) → 新建的 `TerminalOutput` 把四个 G 集设为 ASCII(`:16-24`)、`_glSetNumber` 默认 0(`terminalOutput.hpp:59`) ⇒ **DECSTR 连 SI 一起还原**。代价正落在用户点名的最高优先级层：`smacs` 与 `rmacs` 之间死的程序会把画线集留到会话结束，下一个程序的 `q` 画出 U+2500。证人：host `7112/0`；突变 `decstr-none` ⇒ `checks=7112 fails=4` 且四条逐条点名，最后一条直接把乱码打在日志上（`so an ordinary letter is an ordinary letter again: got 0x2500 want 0x71`）；live 两臂先证"装上了"（`─`）再证"软复位卸掉了"（`q`），双架构 `6193/0`。**入账的判据：引用一个行号区间而漏读区间内的语句，比不引用更像真的** |
| 86 | ⛔ 不做 | host+live |  |  | **`u8`/`u9` 等 caps 声明** |  |  | 旧理由："两个序列都应答，但 jline 里没人读 user8/user9" ‖ 新判定：~~"理由作废，先查证再声明"~~ ⇒ **查证完毕：判决 = 不声明，且工单自己的前提是误读**。四独立理由（逐条复核，两条腿各跑一遍）：(1) **u8 是模式不是串** —— `infocmp -1x xterm-256color` 第 198–201 行给 `u8=\E[?%[;0123456789]c`，而本栈 DA1 是定值 **36 字节** `\x1b[?61;4;6;7;14;21;22;23;24;28;32;42c`（本轮从 `RenderJni.cpp` 的 `case RC_REP_DA` 逐字取出并用 python 解码计数；**旧格子这里抄漏了 `;21` 并写成 33 字节**，两处都已按实测改） ⇒ "声明串 == 实发串"永不可能成立；(2) **u9 语义不是 DA2 应答** —— u9 = `\E[c` 是**主 DA 请求**，把 DA2 应答填进去就是对同一会话另一个解析器撒谎（I26），填诚实的 `\E[c` 则本栈从不发出、也无读者；~~(3) **没有读者** —— `user8` 在整个 jline 里只出现在 `InfoCmp.java:486` 的枚举~~ **【#115 删除：这条不是理由，而且被实测否证】** —— 换一个非 jline 的读者跑一次就塌：WSL 的 ncurses 6.3.20211021 `tput -T xterm-256color u8` **正常输出 `^[[?%[;0123456789]c`（exit 0）**，`u9` 输出 `^[[c`；顺带两条实测细节：长名 `tput user8` 反而报 `unknown terminfo capability`（读者用的是**短名**），`infocmp -1x xterm-256color` 在本机把 `u8=` 落在**第 200 行**、`u9=` 第 201 行（旧格子写的 198–201 是当时那台的行号，引用前该重跑）。**"没有读者"既不是拒绝的合格理由（属主 2026-09-28 第二条裁决），事实上也假**；删掉它**不改变判决**，改变的只是理由的诚实性 —— 剩下三条各自独立成立；(4→3) **声明会弄崩自家 tputs** —— `Curses.java:425` 对 `%[` 直接 `throw new IllegalArgumentException()`。**⚠ #115 顺手订正一处会咬人的引用不精确**：这条锚点在两棵树里**撞名** —— 说的是 `jline3.29/terminal/src/main/java/org/jline/utils/Curses.java:425-426`（`if ("cdoxXs".indexOf(ch) < 0) { throw ... }`，本轮打开逐字确认），而同仓另有一个 `jline3.29/curses/src/main/java/org/jline/curses/Curses.java`，只写 "Curses.java:425" 会让人去开错文件。⚠ 两处曾写错的证据已订正：`infocmp -x 'xterm*'` **不是"不含 u6–u9"**，是**查找失败**（`couldn't open terminfo file .../xterm*.`，`exit=1`，infocmp 不支持通配）；钉 DA 回复字节的**不是 `geo_census`**（host 只钉种类与 FIFO，`gm_reports`），逐字节腿是 live `caseReports()`。另踩一条坑：树里有**两个** `Curses.java`，`curses/…/curses/Curses.java` 只有 262 行、没有那段抛出 ‖ 可行性：落地：`terminfo/windows-conemu.caps` 首行的 u8/u9 那格已改写为四条理由 + 全部改用**函数名锚点**（原先那两个行号 `Render.cpp:1220-1221`/`RenderJni.cpp:397-401` 已腐烂，故这次不留数字）；§11 的本节与 `ANSI_SUPPORTS.md` §3 同步。**不新增门禁**：u8/u9 既不进条目，声明串==实发串 那条臂（#90 的 `cbt` 做法）就无对象可钉，等真有任何一家读者出现再开。 |

The set of answered ids has been growing item by item with #77/#80/#83 (4/6/7/25/47/1047/1049/69/2004/2026); what this row adds is the other half: **no state, no answer**. Eleven ids each "zero replies + one RC_UN_MODE vote", five ids each "must answer + zero votes", both halves in the same leg so they cannot drift. The criterion is the reader's own reading: jline4's `parseDecrpm` looks each reply up by number (AbstractTerminal.java:675-690), so answering one id too many = minting a new I26-style claim, while answering one too few is merely NOT_SUPPORTED -- the two are not symmetric.**This leg is the standing textbook case for "green on the first run is not a witness"**: the first time it was run it was green, so `mode_status` was made to answer 2048 out of thin air and the leg went red at once (`no reply for an id with no state behind it: got 0x1 want 0x0`), and it reddened two **older adjudication legs** already on the tree at the same time ("reflow and in-band resize answer nothing" and "each votes for the mode it asked about") -- which became #82's precondition directly: changing the silence ruling for 2048 obliges you to rewrite those two. After restoring, host `checks=6287 fails=0`; the dll bytes on both architectures match the deployed pair (162675/166905), so nothing was re-deployed. | 项：**DECRQM 的非私有拼法与更多模式号** —— ✅ 已落地 2026-09-27（**不抬 stamp**：只动 `RenderCheck.cpp`，应答集合早被 #77/#80/#83 逐条扩到 4/6/7/25/47/1047/1049/69/2004/2026） ‖ 旧理由："`?` 缺失、两个 interim、`$p` 之外的写法 —— 上游都拒" ‖ 新判定：**逐条重判**：凡是模型真持有状态的号（`?4` 之后有 IRM、`?48` 之后有 DECOM、`?2048`、`?2004`）都该答；纯事件型（`?1048`）与无状态可报者继续沉默，但理由要写成"没有可报状态"，不是"上游不答" ‖ 可行性：落地＝把**沉默集合钉成一条腿**（`geo_decrpm_silence`）：11 个无状态号（1/2/3/9/12/1034/1048/2000/2027/2031/2048）逐个断言"零应答 + 恰好一票 RC_UN_MODE"，另半条腿断言"5 个有状态号必须各答一次且投票为 0"——两半同腿，免得只钉住一边。判据：jline4 `parseDecrpm` 按号回查（AbstractTerminal.java:675-690），多答一个就是谎（I26），少答一个是"不支持"，二者不对称。<br>**一次就绿不算证人**：这条腿第一次跑是绿的，所以当场做突变——让 `mode_status` 凭空答 2048 —— 它立刻红（`no reply for an id with no state behind it: got 0x1 want 0x0`，且 `and exactly one vote` 同步红），同时**顺带把树上两条旧腿也照红了**（"reflow and in-band resize answer nothing"/"each votes for the mode it asked about"），这就是 #82 必须先重写旧裁决腿的实证。恢复后 host `checks=6287 fails=0`（6178→6287 全来自这条枚举腿与其 corpus 复放），两架构 dll 字节回到 162675/166905 = 部署件，**未重部署**（`RenderCheck.cpp` 不进 dll）。 \| |

| 89 | ✅ 已落地 | host+live+code-only |  | `cache/p63/rot75/dbcli.jar.20260926-232946.bak` | Reserve a slot in `stats()` for arguments that were dropped: #74 counted them but nobody on the production path ever read them | `NativeRenderer.java` (`SLOT_ARGTRUNC`/`SLOT_LAST`, the report clause), `Render.java` (`S_ARGTRUNC`, the length gate rewritten as an equality, `reportSlotAgrees`, the two-door leg), `run.ps1` (`-DbcliJar`, `-SkipJarCheck`, the shipped-class leg) | Landed 2026-09-26 with **no build stamp** -- the dll was not rebuilt, and the two shipped natives still read `cecea4ff5528`/`584bc235c081` **-- that snapshot has since been replaced twice: -35 shipped `22e2bfb817b8`/`7b589c799a7b` and the 2026-09-27 #83 carry fix shipped -36 `63a7eb5fc31d`/`bdf6317cc6e5`. The row's own claim (it lifted no stamp) stands; only its census rotted**. The ticket as written was one line ("add a slot") and the truth was three gaps. **One: the slot already existed.** `RenderJni.cpp` has written `STAT_ARGTRUNC` (index 54) since build -28, while `NativeRenderer.SLOT_LAST` and `Render.S_LEN` both stopped at 54 -- so the counter a rollout was supposed to read was in the array and read by nobody. It looked covered because #74 gave the *gate* a private export (`Java_Render_argTrunc`) and every leg used that door. **Two: the length assertion was a floor.** `s.length >= S_LEN` can only see a dll older than the table; it can never see a dll that outgrew it, which is exactly the direction in which a slot is added. Now it is an equality and the message says which side is stale -- the same family as the both-leg `SKIP` and the un-exported `JAR`: a comparing gate has to name the direction it can fail in. **Three, and the one worth keeping:** the live gate never touches `lib\dbcli.jar`. It compiles `NativeRenderer.java` out of the source tree with `-sourcepath` and runs with only its own output directory on the classpath, so *a change to the library's own report sentence is invisible to every gate that exists*. `run.ps1` now compares the class it just ran against the class the shipped jar carries, byte for byte -- sound because the same javac reaches the same bytes from either route (`2B9DF055C608` on both sides), which is what makes a hash the right claim here. Witnesses, all seen this round: writing `S_ARGTRUNC` as 53 turns the two-door leg and the reflection seam red together (`live89arm-red.txt`, `checks=5805 failures=2`); pointing `-DbcliJar` at the pre-#89 backup leaves the gate at `failures=0` and fails only the new jar leg (`live89jar-red.txt`), which is the proof the leg is not a restatement of the gate; reverting and re-running gives x86 and x64 each `checks=5805 failures=0`, `STAGE0 RUN: ok`, and the census line ending `parameters dropped=4`. Deployed with `jar uf` on the single class entry: `lib/dbcli.jar` 682169 bytes `0009e8016f89`, previous copy kept at `cache/p63/rot75/dbcli.jar.20260926-232946.bak`; per the user's ruling the jar is copied into the repo and not committed. One drift corrected on the way: the repo's `src/java/com/hyee/ansirender/NativeRenderer.java` had stopped at 29575 bytes against the authority tree's 30900. | 项：**`stats()` 里给"被丢掉的参数"留一格** —— ✅ 已落地 2026-09-26（**不抬 stamp**：C 侧一个字节没变，出货 dll 仍是 -33 的 `cecea4ff5528`/`584bc235c081`） ‖ 旧理由：#74 把 `push_arg` 丢 surplus 变成了 `nArgTrunc`，但只有 gate-only 导出 `Java_Render_argTrunc` 能读到它 ‖ 新判定：**工单本身比真相小一圈：槽位早就在数组里了，是两个 Java 表没读它。** `RenderJni.cpp` 从 -28 起就写 slot 54（`STAT_ARGTRUNC`），而 `NativeRenderer.SLOT_LAST` 与 `Render.S_LEN` 都停在 54 —— 于是生产路径上那个数字**没人取过**，host/live 两档门禁看起来覆盖，是因为它们走的是 #74 特意为门禁加的那个导出。**第二个洞是门禁的形状**：那句长度检查写的是 `s.length >= S_LEN`，一个下限只看得见"dll 比表旧"，永远看不见"dll 比表长"，所以加槽这件事从头到尾没有一次报警。**第三个洞本轮才暴露**：`run.ps1` 根本不碰 `lib\dbcli.jar` —— 它用 `-sourcepath` 从 `D:\JavaProjects\jline3.29\dbcli\src` 现编 `NativeRenderer` 再用自己的 out 目录跑，因此**改动库自己的 report 句子是没有任何门禁能证伪的**。\| 落地三处 + 一条新臂：`NativeRenderer.java`(`SLOT_ARGTRUNC = 54`, `SLOT_LAST = 55`，report 末句 `N parameter(s) dropped by a CSI list longer than the parser holds`)、`Render.java`(`S_ARGTRUNC`、长度断言改成**等值**、`reportSlotAgrees("SLOT_ARGTRUNC")`、两扇门同一数字的 leg)、`run.ps1`(`-DbcliJar`/`-SkipJarCheck`，比较门自己跑的 class 与 jar 里的 class，逐字节)。同一 javac 在 `-sourcepath`/`-implicit:class` 与手敲命令下产出**同一字节**（两侧都 `2B9DF055C608…`），所以哈希就是判据。证人三次现场红：把 `S_ARGTRUNC` 写成 53 ⇒ 两扇门 + 反射臂一起红（`live89arm-red.txt`, `checks=5805 failures=2`）；把 jar 指回 #89 之前的备份 ⇒ 门禁 0 失败而 JAR CHECK 报 DRIFT（`live89jar-red.txt`）；恢复后 x86/x64 各 `checks=5805 failures=0`、`STAGE0 RUN: ok`、报表行末尾 `parameters dropped=4`。部署：`jar uf` 换 `com/hyee/ansirender/NativeRenderer.class` 单条目，`lib/dbcli.jar` 682169 / `0009e8016f89`，改前备份 `cache/p63/rot75/dbcli.jar.20260926-232946.bak`；按新裁决 jar 只拷贝不提交。顺带纠一处漂移：仓库里 `src/java/com/hyee/ansirender/NativeRenderer.java` 停在 29575 字节、权威树 30900 —— 本轮一并同步 ‖ 可行性： |
| 90 | ✅ 已落地 | host+code-only |  | `cache/p63/rot75/jline-before-cbt.jar` | Put `cbt` into the terminfo entry: the debt #70 owed, and the first time "move all three copies together" actually moved | `terminfo/windows-conemu.caps` + `windows-conemu.ti` + `infocmp-windows-conemu.txt` (regenerated), `CapsDump.java` (three legs and a reader leg), `TERMINFO.md`; `lib/JLine3.jar` and jline's source + `target/classes` copies **copied, not committed** | Landed 2026-09-26 with **no build stamp**, because `render.dll` did not change. One capability went in and two stayed out, and the split is the finding: `cbt`'s only consumer in this stack reads it as the **name of the Shift-Tab key** (`LineReaderImpl.java:6420` binds `REVERSE_MENU_COMPLETE` to `key(Capability.back_tab)`; `KeyMap.key` is `Curses.tputs(getStringCapability(..))` at `:342`, and `KeyMap.bind` at `:589` drops a null key sequence **without a word**), while `hts`/`tbc` have no reader at all -- `Capability.set_tab` and `Capability.clear_all_tabs` occur nowhere outside `InfoCmp.java`'s own table. So omitting `cbt` was never a refusal to claim; it was a silent unbind. The bytes were already arriving: the Windows key leg synthesises them from `kcbt` at `AbstractWindowsTerminal.java:368` and decodes the file spelling at `:441`. That asymmetry sharpened the entry's own rule, which had been written as one flat AND: the rule governs the strings **this stack writes**, and a name a reader only uses to decode bytes the host sends is judged by whether the host sends them -- which is why `cbt` is in and `smkx` and `hts` are not. Two sentences in the older rows are now marked wrong: the citation `LineReaderImpl.java:6992` is jline4's line, not this tree's (`:6420`), and the claim that dbcli did not lose the key because `Console.java:295` binds `^[Z` is false -- jline's key syntax reads `^[` as one ESC, so that binding is ESC+Z, two bytes, a *different* chord from `ESC [ Z`. Proof: `caps_check.ps1` prints `CAPS CHECK: ok` on both its runs (87 assertions, up from 81) and `terminfo_check.sh` prints `TERMINFO CHECK: ok` with all four arms green and **91** capabilities matching across `.ti`/mirror/jar (`1f3e082c3f12`). The new reader leg is the one worth the ticket: it builds jline's headless `LineDisciplineTerminal` with type `windows-conemu`, which loads the entry **off the classpath** the way a real session does (`getInfoCmp` skips the `infocmp` subprocess on Windows at `:604` and falls to the bundled resource), then asks the reader's own `menu` keymap what Shift-Tab binds to. It reads `reverse-menu-complete` against the shipped jar and `no cbt to look up` against the pre-#90 jar kept at `cache/p63/rot75/jline-before-cbt.jar` -- red and green on the same program, which is what makes it a witness rather than a restatement. (Its first version printed `unbound (null)` for a reason that had nothing to do with the entry: the keymap is registered under `LineReader.MENU`, whose value is the lowercase `"menu"`.) Moving the three copies together needed no rebuild: `jar uf` replaced the single entry in `lib/JLine3.jar` (the jar is unsigned -- `META-INF/MANIFEST.MF` and no `.SF`), and the byte compare that leg exists for came back identical. Per the user's ruling of 2026-09-26, `lib/JLine3.jar` and `lib/*/render.dll` are **copied into the repo and never committed**; the repo's own jar still carries upstream's 1078-byte entry, and that drift is the user's to settle. | 项：**把 `cbt`/`hts`/`tbc` 加进 terminfo 条目** —— ✅ 已落地 2026-09-26（**不抬 stamp**：`render.dll` 一个字节没变） ‖ 旧理由：#70 已经把 `ESC H`、`CSI Ps g`、`CSI Ps I`、`CSI Ps Z` 全实现了，条目里仍然一条都不声明 ‖ 新判定：**三条不是一条：只有 `cbt` 进，`hts`/`tbc` 留外面，而分界就是"有没有读者"。** `cbt` 的唯一消费者把它当 **Shift-Tab 的键名**读（`LineReaderImpl.java:6420`，本树行号；先前引的 `:6992` 是 jline4），`KeyMap.bind`（`:589`）遇到 null 静默不绑 —— 所以少声明它不是"拒绝一个主张"，是**默默解绑一个键**；字节本来就在发（`kcbt` → `AbstractWindowsTerminal.java:368`，`:441` 解码）。而 `hts`/`tbc` 在整棵 jline 里除了 `InfoCmp.java` 的表没有任何出现，写出去又是对另一个解析器的谎（CEAnsi 丢 `ESC H`/`CSI 3g`）。条目第一句的 AND 规则就此收窄：**管的是本栈写出去的串**，只用来解码宿主字节的按名目判 ‖ 可行性：三副本逐字节对齐（镜像 / jline 源码与 `target/classes` / `lib/JLine3.jar`，jar 无签名 ⇒ `jar uf` 换单条目，md5 `1f3e082c3f12`）；门禁：`caps_check.ps1` 两跑 `CAPS CHECK: ok`（断言 81→87），`terminfo_check.sh` 四臂 ok（能力 90→91）。新增的 reader 臂跑在真 jar 上：对 #90 之前的 jar 报 `no cbt to look up`（红），对出货 jar 报 `reverse-menu-complete`（绿）—— 这条红绿同源，才算证人。按用户 2026-09-26 裁决，jar 与 dll 只**拷贝**进仓库、不提交 |

每条都断言了现行词表，必须一起改口，并且要留下一条"翻转后老断言整片红"的臂——翻过去仍然绿，就说明那些腿从来没在断言语义。**同一条待查挂在 #93 里**：`?2048` 在 DECSTR 后答 not-set 的依据也写成 `SoftReset` 的名单，而那份名单（`:2989`）里没有 2048；#82 当时的判据要重读 ⚠ `:659-666` 的 javadoc **逐位相同**。⇒ "发 3/4 必骗一方"这个论证作废；不发 3/4 的**决定仍然对**，理由换成拿得出的那条：`parseDecrpm:683-685` 把 3 也判 SUPPORTED ⇒ 答 3 就是替一个我们**没有永久事实**的模式做能力主张（I26），而 4 与沉默同效 ⇒ 那个数买不到东西。**已用主文档核实（2026-09-27，经代理 127.0.0.1:10808 抓 xterm ctlseqs，HTTP 200 / 293655 字节，存 `cache/audit90/xterm_ctlseqs.html`，摘录 `cache/audit90/t7_xterm_decrqm.py` 的输出）**：xterm 文档里 DECRPM 的 Pm 逐字是 `0 not recognized / 1 set / 2 reset / 3 permanently set / 4 permanently reset`，**与 MSFT `DispatchTypes.hpp:555-558` 和 jline4 `:659-666` 三方一致**。⇒ 原注释那句"xterm 和 DEC 把 4 读成 permanently set、jline4 把 3 写成那样"不仅两家不相反，**连它举的那个例子本身也是反的**（xterm 明写 3 = permanently set）。今天没有任何一个读者与另一个读者在这个数上分歧。**这条与 1/2 那个反转同源**：都是拿一个没有打开过的参照关系给已经发出去的字节当理由。 —— ✅ **已修 = build -46**。 判据（数字都来自命令输出）：host `checks=7114 fails=0`；live 双架构各 `checks=6193 failures=0` + `STAGE0 RUN: ok`； **出货字节副本**（`-Scratch cache/ship46`）再跑一遍，双架构又 `6193/0`。dll x86 183702 / `4720d1485cde7afbdb4ea829a85a52eb`、 x64 184787 / `906f3ab93e23fa28f2208cb99f338d47` —— **字节换了而两个尺寸与 -45 一字不变**，是【尺寸不是身份】又一例。 -45 备份 `cache/audit90/render-*.20260927-185048.bak`。 红臂两支各证一头（lab `cache/audit90/t7mut/`，脚本 `t7_mut93.py`）：**`one25`** 只把 `case 25` 翻回旧词表 ⇒ `fails=2`，两条都点名光标可视那一（`a fresh grid reports the cursor visible` 与 `and `?25l` is a fact the reply carries`）； **`all12`** 把十个 case 全翻回去 ⇒ `fails=52`，逐族点名（鼠标九号的 set 臂与 reset 臂、DECAWM、IRM、DECOM、DECLRMM、 2004/2026/2048、屏族三条同槽、DECSTR/RIS 复位臂）。⇒ 断言确实在断言这个数，不是在数回复条数。 ⚠ **改口过程本身抓到两条判据**。(1) 批量翻转的脚本按 `it.status, N` 匹配，而**三处**读同一个字段的断言是通过别的 局部名读的（`second.status`、`alt.status`、`legacy.status`）—— 第一次跑就漏成三条红。**按变量名 grep 做机械改口， 漏的必然是那些语义相同、写法不同的地方**；改口类编辑要按【值形状】匹配（`status, <数字>`）。 (2) 我这轮新加的臂**自己也错了**：`geo_decrpm_silence` 的载荷是**先设后问**，那六号的正确答案是 1（set）不是 2， 它第一次跑就红 ⇒ 那是腿错、不是代码错，红得有道理 —— 一个刚写的臂在替被测对象说话之前，先要证明它肯替反方说话。 | 项：**DECRPM 的 1/2 语义与 DEC/MSFT 相反**（#81 把它扩到九个新模式时才被抓到）—— ✅ **已修 = build -46** ‖ 旧理由："jline4 把 1/2/3 一律当 的 javadoc 写的正是正确词表）⇒ "对唯一的读者无害"是为一种还没有读者的能力辩护，不是为正确辩护：任何 `tput`/vim/tmux 问一次就拿到反答案。#81 把这套词表从 10 个模式扩到 19 个，所以必须在这一轮入账 ‖ 可行性：改动小（`mode_status` 全部 `? 2 : 1` 翻转 + 更正 `:1540` 那句），**贵在腿**：host **没有闪烁位** —— `conattrs.hpp:9` 的 `META_ATTRS` 只有 lead/trail、三条 grid line、reverse、underscore；MSFT 的 `Blinking = 0x04`（`:17`）属**内部** `TextAttribute` 空间、与 `FOREGROUND_RED` 同值，出 wincon 前过 `USED_META_ATTRS` 掩码（`TextAttribute.hpp:63-64`）。看着空的 0x0400/0x0800/0x1000 会被 conhost 真画成网格线（`renderer.cpp:1388-1411`→`gdi/paint.cpp:516`），0x2000 被掩掉 ⇒ **存了位也没地方显示**。三家逐一看过：wezterm 真闪，靠自绘面插值 + 自续帧（`wezterm-cell/src/lib.rs:207`、`screen_line.rs:824-850`、速率 `termwindow/mod.rs:769-782`）；ghostty **存而不绘**（`sgr.zig:302/304/320`、`style.zig:31-42`、`Screen.zig:2368-2374`，而 `src/renderer/` 整棵树 "blink" 只有 `renderer/cursor.zig`——本轮亲验）；MSFT 把 blink 折进 faint（`RenderSettings.cpp:192`、`renderer.cpp:815-830`），屏幕上与 SGR 2 不可分。**ghostty 的"存而不绘"不替我们背书**：它的存位对它自己的读者有用（选择/复制、style 序列化），而本库唯一的 per-cell 对外通道是 `ReadConsoleOutputW` 回读（`RenderJni.cpp:2326-2357`），attr 到 conhost 已被吞、Java 看不见 ⇒ "只存位不画"在这里连对外报告都够不着，要做就得新设侧信道（`ANSI_SUPPORTS.md:207` 那类"收养即丢"先例），那是另立项。用户授权原文："blink 如果和其他特性在代码层面有冲突，那先查下 MSFT/GHOSTTY/WEZTERM 代码是怎么实现的，如果解决不了也可以放弃" —— 结论是**冲突不可解**，按授权放弃 | 项：**SGR 5/6/25（闪烁）** —— **判决：不做**（2026-09-27 查证后关单，不是搁置） ‖ 旧理由："上游 ConEmu 遇 `CSI 5m` 只清反向、不留状态" ‖ 新判定：**决定性事实在 Windows 这一侧**：本库最终写出去的是 `CHAR_INFO.Attributes`，而这个 WORD 里**没有闪烁位** —— `conattrs.hpp:9` 的 `META_ATTRS` 全集只有 lead/trail、三条 grid line、reverse、underscore；MSFT 自己的 `Blinking = 0x04`（`:17`）属**内部** `TextAttribute` 空间、与 `FOREGROUND_RED` 同值，出 wincon 前要过 `TextAttribute.hpp:63-64` 的 `USED_META_ATTRS` 掩码。看着空的 0x0400/0x0800/0x1000 会被 conhost 真画成网格线（`renderer.cpp:1388-1411` → `gdi/paint.cpp:516`、`:600`），0x2000 直接被掩掉 ⇒ **存了位也没地方显示**，声称实现就是撒谎 ‖ 可行性：三家逐一看过（本轮亲验）：**wezterm 真闪**——`Sgr::Blink(Slow\|Rapid\|None)`(`csi.rs:2760-2762`) → pen(`terminalstate/mod.rs:2794-2795`) → 属性字 5/6 两位(`wezterm-cell/src/lib.rs:207`) → 在 bg↔fg 间插值并自续下一帧(`screen_line.rs:824-850`，慢/快两档速率 `termwindow/mod.rs:769-782`)，靠的是**自绘面**，不是 conhost。**ghostty 存而不绘**——`sgr.zig:302/304/320` 把 5/6→`.blink`、25→`.reset_blink`，`style.zig:31-42` 存进 packed u16 第 3 位、`Screen.zig:2368-2374` 落到格，但 `src/renderer/` 整棵树里 "blink" 只有 `renderer/cursor.zig`（光标自己的闪），逐格 blink 0 命中。**MSFT 半真**——一个 1 秒全局定时器把 blink 折进 faint（`RenderSettings.cpp:192`、`renderer.cpp:815-830`），屏幕上与 SGR 2 不可分 **关单，三条硬事实留下**：(1) 本库没有 conhost 之外的显示面；(2) "存而不绘"在这里连对外报告都够不着——唯一的 per-cell 外报通道是 `ReadConsoleOutputW` 回读（`RenderJni.cpp:2326-2357`），attr 到 conhost 已被吞、Java 看不见，要让它可见等于新设侧信道（`ANSI_SUPPORTS.md:207` 那类"收养即丢"的先例），那是另立项不是加位；(3) 现有文档一致断言两腿都不存状态（`terminfo/windows-conemu.ti:27`、`DESIGN.md:230`、`ANSI_TODO.md:125`），本轮**不改任何代码**，`Render.cpp:974` 的 `case 5: case 6: case 25: break;` 原样保留。用户裁决原文："blink 如果和其他特性在代码层面有冲突，那先查下 MSFT/GHOSTTY/WEZTERM 代码是怎么实现的，如果解决不了也可以放弃"——查证结论是**冲突不可解**（Windows 属性字没有位），按这条授权放弃 |

1"的断言，栅格回读，不看 screen 字符串。 **2026-09-28 本会话补测（-50）**：`cache/t5-ship46/leg98wt.json` 那条腿**到不了它要量的状态**，而且原因本身是新事实。① 在 WT 里 `scroll` 步是空操作（视口本来就在 (0,0)，WT 的 scrollback 不归 conhost 的窗口 API 管）；② 在经典 conhost 里也同样是空操作——试了四种产出方式（`help` 分页一屏、`set pagesize 0` 后 `help`、25 条单行命令 `expr 6*7`、40 次空回车），**窗口五次都停在 `window_rect=[0,0,99,29]`**，而 `buffer=[2000,9001]`（`cache/adopt49/s98*/json.txt`、`s98d`、`s98e`）。⇒ 渲染器自己持有滚动并把**同一批窗口行**重画，控制台缓冲区从不被滑下去，所以"用户往上滚回历史"在这个宿主组合下**没有可滚的对象**——这条工单原先假设的"视口滚动之后"需要先解决"历史到底存在哪一层"。③ 顺带：`help` 被分页器停在一屏（`VARIABLE VAR` 那行是最后一页的末行），这解释了属主 1:30 那张实拍里提示符为什么在列表之后。⇒ #98 的复现要么改用真滚轮/UIA（WT 侧），要么先回答"渲染器的 scrollback 深度 = 模型 gutter，与宿主缓冲区无关"这个归属问题；**在有人解决之前，本行仍算未做，且不许引用 leg98wt 说它复现过**。另：#101(a) 的修复（`render()` 在 FLUSH_NOGEOM 且整块未落时重建并重试同一 chunk）**没有**改变本行的形状——本行是 scroll 触发，#101 是 resize 触发。 |

ANSI_RENDER_LIB` 0 命中、`libDir` 0 命中，只剩 `ANSI_RENDER`（`isEnabled()`，属主没否）。⚠ **本行不是删前/删后的 A/B，也不许被读成那样**：删前只有两支 WT 臂跑过、且用的还是没有 `PTY=`/`WINSELF=` 的旧探针，五臂矩阵是删之后重打的。它不需要是 A/B —— 那个变量在五次运行里都是 `null`，被删的代码路径不可达，可达性由报告自证。文档改口：`DESIGN.md:138-142`、`ANSI_TODO.md` #100、`JLINE_CHANGES.md` 的 #99 节；长期记忆 `project-conemu-writer-perf.md:771` 那条改名账也要跟着改口，否则它会教后来者去找一个不存在的变量 | 项：**删掉环境变量 `ANSI_RENDER_LIB`**（属主 2026-09-27 两次点名） ‖ 旧理由："`loadLibrary` 失败时给一个显式目录兜底" ‖ 新判定：**属主裁决：没有这种问题，不需要这个变量**（原话"ANSI_RENDER_LIB完全就是没必要的，不要设一堆的变量，非常不友好"）。按文件核过它确实只服务于一个造不出来的失败：`NativeRenderer.java` 里它只在 `System.loadLibrary("render")` 抛 `UnsatisfiedLinkError` 之后才被读，而启动器本就把 `lib\x64`/`lib\x86` 放进 PATH，五臂矩阵**全程未设这个变量**、渲染器照样 `ENGAGED`。留下的只有成本：每多一个变量，用户就多一件必须知道的事。**已删**（`libDir()` 连同两处分支），错误句改成点名两条真实通道 —— `render.dll could not be loaded from java.library.path or PATH: …`，并且**不许**在它只是架构不符时谎称"文件不存在"（`UnsatisfiedLinkError` 两种失败共用一个异常类，这是注释里写明的事） ‖ 可行性：编译产物自证：`javap -p -c NativeRenderer.class` 里 `String ANSI_RENDER_LIB` 0 命中、`libDir` 0 命中、只剩 `String ANSI_RENDER`（`isEnabled()` 那个开关，属主没否）。行为自证：**不许**把本行读成删前/删后的 A/B —— 删前只有两支 WT 臂跑过、用的还是没有 `PTY=`/`WINSELF=` 的旧探针；五臂矩阵是删之后重打的，`RENDER=ENGAGED` 三支（`classic-clean`、`classic-fake`、`wt-noenv`）、`windows-vtp/off` 两支（`wt-native`、`wt-noenv-old`），且五次运行时该变量都被 `cell.cmd` 显式清空（那一行已随本条一起删掉）——被删的路径在五次运行里都不可达，这才是本行的证人形状。文档改口：`DESIGN.md:138-142`（那句"can name the DLL explicitly"换成"只有一个开关 + 两条通道"并记下删它的理由）；`.dsh/memory/project/project-conemu-writer-perf.md:771` 那条"库路径 `DBCLI_RENDER_LIB` → `ANSI_RENDER_LIB`"的改名账要一起改口，否则长期记忆会教后来者去找一个不存在的变量 |

jline 的 WINCH 重画（CR + 擦到屏末 + CUP + 提示符）既没被画也没被解析，提示符就此消失。修法：`FLUSH_NOGEOM` **只在整块都没落的时候**才返回（落了的话 `DECLINE` 宏答的是 `FLUSH_RESYNC`），所以字节是完整的 ⇒ 就地 `readopt()` 按控制台现有形状重建、把**同一 chunk 再上一次**（`RenderJni.cpp::render`，注释写明为什么安全、以及为什么不改成"每 chunk 先问一次形状"：那要给每个 chunk 加一次 ~78 us 的控制台调用）。**证人四件**：① 新腿 `Render.java::caseWinchPrompt` **走生产路径**（反射调私有 `render()`，因为 adopt/decline/retry A future reader should not "fix" that by inventing a gate that cannot fail.<br>The honest gaps in the table, stated rather than papered over. **Rows #13 and #14 are dbcli's pager and have no<br>`render.dll` gate at any level** -- their only witness is a live console leg, which is weaker than the rest of<br>this section and is the first thing to re-run if the pager misbehaves after a jline change; #14's leg is at least<br>doubled by a `javap` reading of the shipped bytecode, #13's is not. **Row #13's `ceon` leg is unreadable as<br>witnessed** (the harness's own prompt regex cannot parse a grid that still holds 83 literal escapes), so the row<br>rests on one leg, not two. **Row #16's ruling now has a produced A/B, and it is the wrong A/B**: what<br>`wide1516-20260925-13.txt` measures is *line width* across two geometries, not a wide-buffer scroll, so §9's<br>scroll leg is still owed and deviation 1 still rests on the planner's algebra. And **row #23's legs and the -6<br>file they replace were run against different jars** (`2297d517…` vs `0ea9b33c…`), so the two agree on the shape<br>of the status rows and nothing about Java-side behaviour. Three more, from the 2026-09-25 rows: **#37 and #38<br>have no real-ConEmu-window leg** -- `NativeWinSysTerminal` also reports `windows-conemu` when `ConEmuPID` is<br>set, so the third host in this family is unmeasured and only inferred from the two that were. **#39's snap leg<br>proves the call is safe, not that it helps** -- **overturned 2026-09-27 by reading the leg that already ships.**<br>`Render.java:3475 caseSnapOnInput` scrolls the window to the buffer top, asserts the prompt row is *below* the<br>viewport, calls `snap()`, then reads the window back through `consoleView()` and asserts `v[0] + WIN_H - 1 == want`<br>(the least slide that reveals the cursor's row) and `v[6] == want`, plus "the snap wrote none of the N scrollback<br>rows", no cell painted, no re-adopt, no flush, `snaps == snaps0 + 1`. Both arches print it green at -36<br>(`cache/audit90/live-gate.txt`). The "useful" half was in the standing gate all along and this sentence was stale. And **#40's witness is a probe harness,<br>not a gate** -- the fix is in dbcli's `Console.java`, so neither standing `render.dll` gate can carry it. The<br>same applies to the CJK-doubling re-check that followed it (`cache/wt-doubling-ab-20260925`): a real Windows<br>Terminal window read `0 doubled pair(s)` with 14 filler rows intact at `frames_read=192`, and the classic<br>conhost leg where the renderer *is* engaged read the same `0 doubled pair(s)` at `frames_read=134`, but only<br>the shipped arm was taken through that harness -- the pre-fix arm rests on #37's ENGAGED/off matrix, not on a<br>same-harness before/after. **Seven more, from the rows added since:** **#48 has a unit red/green pair and no<br>post-fix live arm** -- the symptom was watched in a real window and reproduced there, but what closed it is<br>`StatusTest`'s six cases (`tests=6 found=6 succeeded=6 failed=0`) plus a single-block revert as the control<br>(`succeeded=4 failed=2`, and the two red are exactly the bordered-resize cases); a library-side proof for a<br>symptom that only ever shows in a window. **#43 is stated as mechanism-removed, not symptom-witnessed**, for the<br>reason given below. **#44's proof depends on a hook that ships**: `Java_Render_faultRect` is in `build.sh`'s<br>export whitelist because the gate links against the released DLL, so `render.dll` exports a fault injector that<br>nothing at runtime calls -- said here rather than buried, because it is the kind of seam a later reader otherwise<br>assumes is dead weight. **#45 is a gap that is not about evidence** -- #38's classification fix is still not in<br>`lib/`, and until it is, no ConEmu-window leg can be taken at all. **(Both halves of that sentence are false as of<br>2026-09-27: `lib/dbcli.jar` carries `org/dbcli/WinSysTerminal.class` at 17087 bytes with `pseudoConsole`,<br>`ANSICON_DEF`, `WT_SESSION`, `windows-conemu` and `snapOnInput` in its constant pool, which is exactly what #38<br>ruled should ship. So the blocker is gone and the only remaining half of #45 is the one that was never measured:<br>no ConEmu-window classification leg exists.)** **#53's own list is still open at one item**,<br>and #51 blocks on it: the interim marker has a single slot where DECRQM/DECRPM need a set. And **#47/#48 leave<br>their library half un-rehearsed** -- `JLINE_CHANGES.md`'s "What is not witnessed" is the list (`reset()` has no<br>red/green pair, `resize(Size)` has no unit case of its own), because those are edits to someone else's codebase<br>and this file does not get to claim their coverage. If a row's witness file is missing, the row is<br>unproven, whatever the two standing gates say.<br>**Row #41's two claims are both answered, one by invalidating a witness and one by removing a mechanism.** The<br>20-column staircase was never on the screen. #42 shows it to be the probe's `ReadConsoleOutputW` readback of<br>conhost's *shadow* buffer, which keeps the pre-resize pitch while the window itself renders clean -- one fixture<br>run through two resize paths, with `uia-resize.ps1` as the only qualified resize witness and `pw-shot.ps1`<br>(`PrintWindow`) as the witness to what a user sees. The trigger is named and it is the harness, and the rule that<br>survives the finding is in `.dsh/memory/reference/reference-wt-geometry-witness.md`: on a `--host wt` leg a<br>readback is not evidence about geometry. The second claim -- close the bar, *then* resize, and the screen wipes to<br>one prompt row -- is #43, where the mechanism was a scroll region a torn-down bar left behind and the removal is<br>`Status.reset()` writing `CSI r` with no parameters. 回读确认，不是假设）只产出 **1** 条记录（ConEmu 3 条）；同一轮里 `MENU_EVENT` 收了 **12** 条（我那 12 次 ALT tap）——这既是"读队列的腿确实在读"的对照，也是"焦点记录的投递与切换不是一比一"的事实。谁要把它们翻成 `\E[I`/`\E[O`，就必须待在那层能合并、能排序的地方。**③ 归属改了，但结论不变**：`?1004` 不再是 ① "没有承载面"，而是 **③ 类冲突**——本库不读 CONIN$（#83 偷键地板 / #95 借还 / #104 借宿主解析器），代价写明在本行与 §5：**本库接管输出时 `?1004h` 被鼠标族自己存位（`Render.cpp:1612` `case 1004`），到不了 conhost 的解析器，所以宿主那条"VT-input 下自己翻成字节"的路是被本库掐断的**（不是宿主不会翻）。**④ 那条未证臂已跑，结果是负的**（2026-09-28，`cache/f106/real3-vt0.json` / `real3-vt1.json`，两臂同一经典 conhost 窗口、只差输入模式）：探针改用 `GetStdHandle(-11)` 之后 `?1004h` 真的写进去了（`written 8/8`、`borrowed_vtp true`、`mode_after_borrow 7`、`vtp_read_back true`，输入模式 `503 → 1015` = 挂了 `ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_INPUT`）。**两臂都还是 `FOCUS_EVENT` 记录、都没有 `\E[I`/`\E[O` 文本**（`counts` 里 `focus: 1` 两臂相同，`vt_focus_sequences [0,0]`，`key_text` 空）⇒ 本机这台 conhost **没有**把 VT-input 下的焦点切换翻成文本；源码那条 `inputBuffer.cpp:585-600` 的双分支在这条会话上只观察到 record 那一支。⚠ **这条负结果带着一个自己造成的混淆，不许藏**：探针按 #104 的借还纪律写完 `?1004h` 立刻把输出模式**还回 base**（VTP 关掉），而 `Mode::FocusEvent` 是输出解析器在 VTP 开着时才置的那个状态——**"关掉 VTP 之后这个模式位还在不在"本轮没有量**，所以严格说这条臂证的是"在 `?1004h` 已被写入、随后 VTP 归还的会话里，焦点仍然以记录到达"，不是"VT-input 永远不出文本"。去混淆的下一臂**配方写在这里、代码还没改**（本轮最后一次打补丁尝试在 argparse 锚点上断言失败，没落笔）：给 `focusprobe.py` 加一个 `--keepvtp` 开关跳过 `K.SetConsoleMode(out_h, out_before)` 那一行，两臂之差只有"还不还 VTP"。**这条负结果把 #106 的理由换掉了**：原判"本库接管输出时 `?1004h` 到不了 conhost 的解析器，所以宿主那条现成路被本库掐断"仍然成立（那是"为什么本库不发字节"的归属理由），但**不能再说"宿主本来会翻成文本、只是被我们挡住"** —— 挡住了什么没被证实。判决（① 本库不发焦点字节）**不变**，它现在靠的是 #108/#116 那条"对面已有编码器且缺的只是调用者"，比这条推断结实。**另一个必须记下的收获是探针自己的假阴性**：旧臂的 `key_text` 由 `32 <= ch < 0xFFFF` 过滤，**把 ESC(27) 整个丢掉** ⇒ `vt_focus_sequences` 永远数不到 `\E[I`，那对 `[0,0]` 是采集器的性质而不是宿主的性质。已改成收全部非零码元（`patch_collector.py`），改前改后各跑一遍才有上面那组数。旧记录里那句"`vt_focus_sequences` 全是 `[0,0]` 因此**不能**被读成 VT 模式不产出序列"现在有了更硬的理由：**它当时根本不具备产出非零值的能力**。 |

## 4 契约与判据（写代码前必读，与表内判决同权）

1. **宽度的两个神谕并存**：2027 探测拿到 NOT_SUPPORTED 后 jline4 用自己的 `WCWidth`+图位簇分组算宽，render.dll 用
   `ansi_width` 表；分歧已由用户裁决（见 §3 的 #49/#61 行与 `ANSI_SUPPORTS.md`）。补特性时不要引入第三种宽度口径。
2. **探测批的收口依赖 DA1**：jline4 靠"DA1 应答到达"结束探测等待，而本库的应答只在**成功 flush 末尾**写入 CONIN$（I29）。
   这条时序不能破坏，否则探测挂死——凡是新增应答，都走同一个 flush 队首。
3. **一个模式位有五处义务**：存位 / 动作 / 应答 / 复位 / 跨重建携带，**每处都要有自己的腿**。#83 的
   `bracketedPaste` 携带缺陷就是只补了前三处而没写"携带"的腿，结果对外报出一个假答案（I26 级）。
4. **动作在 seam 里的特性，证人必须做在 live**：#82 的四臂突变 host 全绿、只有 live 红 ⇒ 同一模式位的"动作"与"应答"
   不住在同一档门禁，host 档看不到 `RenderJni.cpp` 那层。
5. **一条腿如果只能靠"读到一个数字变了"通过，而 harness 没有制造那个状态的手段，它就是没跑**（#39 的 snap 腿缺
   `scroll` 臂，至今未跑，别记成"欠时间"，是欠工具）。
6. **一次就绿不算证人**：落地时当场做突变，让腿看见红（#87 让 `mode_status` 凭空答 2048 ⇒ 立刻红；#84 三臂各咬一类缺陷）。
   突变实验室 `cache/audit90/t6mut/` 之类只拷部分目录时，**判决读 `fails=`，不读进程退出码**——未突变树也可能 rc≠0。
7. **"上游也不做"不是理由**：要找的是**句子里主语是别人**的拒绝句——"ConEmu 也不做""jline 没人读""本树没人发""没有消费者"
   是同一句话的四种写法。每条"不做"必须能答出四者之一：没有承载面 / 会对外撒谎（I26）/ 没有写手（I18）/ 参照也不做。
   工具：`cache/upstream-excuse/cand.py`。逐条重论证的结果全在 §3 的 `工单（原 §10）` 一栏。
8. **两条自己制造过的障碍，别再制造**：① 一格回答了两个问题里"没被问的那个"，比失败更糟（`wt-real` 那格清了
   `WT_SESSION`，测的是"WT+变量被抹掉"却被记成"宿主自己的环境"）；② **文档里的 size/md5 必须来自当次
   `md5sum`+`stat`，不能由人打字**；live 日志要读 `render build:` 那行，`ok` 不证跑的是哪份字节。
9. **`.cmd`/`.bat` 正文一律纯 ASCII**：代码页 936 下一个 UTF-8 `⚠` 的悬空首字节会吞掉后一个字符，
   `cmd.exe` 直接 `exit=255` 且报告文件根本没写，腿就变成"NO REPORT"。
10. **本库是第三方类库，调用方不止 jline4 与 dbcli**（属主 2026-09-28 裁决，2026-09-26 那条的第二次加码）：
    一条拒绝要活下来必须落在四类之一并写明是哪类 —— **①没有承载面 ②会对外撒谎(I26) ③与其他特性/代码实现冲突
    （必须点名冲突的编号与代价，例 #49 的 `?u` 门、#104 借掉宿主 VTP、#83 读 CONIN$ 偷键、#103 只发差分）
    ④安全地板(I21)/属主定案**。
    **"调用方不用 / jline 里没人读 / 本树没人发 / 没有消费者"是第五种说法，它不是理由。**
    逐条重判的开工单在 `TODO_0928.md`（T-07..T-10），台账里的总单是 **#76（至今 🔲 未做）**。

## 5 明确不做（每条的理由必须能重新过一遍）

⛔ **本节原来叫"明确不做（记录防重开）"，属主 2026-09-28 否决了那种写法。** "防重开"把一张表变成了锁：它的功能是
让下一个人**不去查**，而这一轮的两次翻案（#108 的"报告永久不做"、#116 的"对面只差一个调用者"）恰恰只有"去查"才能发现。
现在的口径是相反的：**本节不禁止任何人重开任何一行**；它只要求每一行带着一个**今天仍然站得住、且能被别人重算**的理由，
而"重开一次并把理由换对"永远是合法动作（§4 第 10 条）。一行如果只剩标题里没有理由的那半截，它就**不该存在于本节**，
要么补理由要么变成工单。

口径：本表只收**渲染器/库层**"判决为不做"的条目；terminfo 条目"哪个名字为什么不进 `windows-conemu`"由
`terminfo/windows-conemu.caps` 行尾那份 kept-out 清单与 `TERMINFO.md` 的对应行持有，此处不重复。
每行必须给出**成立的理由**（"上游不做""没人使用""读者在对面"都不算理由，见 §4 第 7 条与第 10 条）与出处；重排前的旧表逐字留在**附录 A**。
**本次复核里被推翻或理由站不住、需要动手的那部分，已拆成同目录的 `TODO_0928.md`（T-01..T-10，含证据锚点与验收门禁）**；
其中 T-07..T-10 来自属主 2026-09-28 的第二条裁决（见 §4 第 10 条：**"调用方不用/没有读者"不是理由**）。
本节只保留复核后**仍然成立**的拒绝口径——而"仍然成立"是对**写下那一轮**的判断，不是对读者的禁令。

| 项 | 判决 | 成立的理由 | 出处 | 判于 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 鼠标**报告**——编码器已落地，**自主投递**仍不做 | ✅ **报告编码器已做**（#108 = build -54），别再把这一行整个读成"不做"；⛔ 仍不做的只剩"本库自己当那个读记录的人" | **已做的一半**：`rc_mouse_report`（`Render.cpp:3674`）把一条控制台鼠标记录按 ghostty `src/input/mouse_encode.zig:81/128-172/178-198/200-240` 的口径编成 X10/UTF8/urxvt/SGR/SGR-pixels 五种形状之一，`rc_focus_report`（`:3806`）出 `\E[I`/`\E[O`；答复与报告**共用同一条注入线** `RenderJni.cpp:844 write_input_units`（从 `flush_reports` 抽出，MSFT 同构：`outputStream.cpp:43-45` 收、`inputBuffer.cpp:814` 发）。五处义务齐全：存位（`Render.cpp:2047`，格式随位于 `:1464`/`:4084`）、动作、应答、复位、**跨重建携带**（`RenderJni.cpp:1643 keepFormat`/`:1648 keepBtn` → `:1707`/`:1711`；`moveSeen` 有意不携带）。五个计数器由 `rc_report_counts`（`Render.cpp:3817`）一次给出，census 与 live 回读共用同一顺序。**没做的一半，理由是 ③ 类且已实测**：本库要成为自己的观察者只有一条路——开 `ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_INPUT`，而 conhost 在那一位打开后把**整个键盘流**交给 `_termInput.HandleKey` 并把结果当**文本**塞回队列（本轮自己打开：`src/host/inputBuffer.cpp:706-715` → `:799-816 _writeString`，其中 `:814` `_storage.push_back(SynthesizeKeyEvent(true, 1, 0, 0, wch, 0))`＝**vk 归 0、控制态归 0、只发 key-down**；映射不上的键落到 `src/terminal/input/terminalInput.cpp:417 _formatFallback`，其 `:1266-1269` 逐字写着 key-up "we don't send anything for those"）。代价＝编辑键/IME/键重复全变文本，与 #83 的粘贴标记、#39/#45 的 snap、属主点名的 top/more/status 正确性正面相冲。**注意路径**：`inputBuffer.cpp` 在 `src/host/` 不在 `src/terminal/adapter/`，早先文档里按后者引的那次是搜不到的那个原因。**投递面已经留好**：JNI 入口 `RenderJni.cpp:2799 Java_Render_mouseReport` / `:2816 Java_Render_focusReport` 各带一个 `inject` 旗子——读控制台记录的那一层递一条进来就出字节，本库不抢队列。`kmous` 进不进条目另判（见 #108 行与 `TERMINFO.md`）：编码器存在≠这个会话会发那些字节 | #81 #95 #108 #116；`TERMINFO.md` 的 `kmous` 行 | 09-27，09-28 复核，~~09-28 裁 ①~~（**该裁决作废**，主语是别人＝第五种无效理由），**09-28 第三轮裁决后落地 = -54** |
| `?1004` 焦点事件的**字节** | ✅ **编得出来、递得进去了**（#108 = build -54）——旧句"仍然只置位不发"作废；⛔ 仍不做的只有"本库自己读 CONIN$ 当观察者"，理由换成 ③ 类 | **编码器** `rc_focus_report`（`Render.cpp:3806`）：模式位清楚时一个字节都不发，置位时发 `\E[I`/`\E[O`。**三神谕本轮逐字打开、口径一致**：MSFT `HandleFocus` 自己也是"只有模式置位才发"（`src/terminal/input/terminalInput.cpp:422-430`，`:424 if (!_inputMode.test(Mode::FocusEvent)) { return MakeUnhandled(); }`）；ghostty `src/terminal/focus.zig:21-24`（`.gained => "\x1B[I"` / `.lost => "\x1B[O"`）；wezterm `term/src/terminalstate/mod.rs:907-909`（`if self.focus_tracking { write!(... "I"/"O") }`）。⇒ 这一条既不是 ① 也没有 ② 的问题。**归谁那一半没变**：把记录翻成字节归"读输入流那一层"，本库不读 CONIN$（#83 偷键地板 / #95 借还纪律 / #104 借宿主解析器同一条约束）；而本库若要自主投递只能开 `ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_INPUT`，代价与鼠标那一行是同一条，已实测（`src/host/inputBuffer.cpp:706-715`→`:814` vk 归 0、`terminalInput.cpp:1266-1269` key-up 不发）⇒ **③ 类，点名编号与代价**，不再是"本库不读 CONIN$"这一句观察。**本库自己造成的后果仍在**：走 render 时 `?1004h` 被本库存位（`Render.cpp:1627`/`:1692`），到不了 conhost 的解析器，宿主那条"VT-input 模式下自己吐 `\E[I`"的路是被本库掐断的。**未证面照旧**：`?1004h` + VT-input ⇒ 记录变文本只有源码依据、没有实测（`cache/f106` 的 `--emit1004` 臂没做成，探针开的 CONOUT$ 不是那条会话的），配方在 #106 行。**投递入口**：`RenderJni.cpp:2816 Java_Render_focusReport(ph, focused, inject)` | #81 #106 #108 #116 | 09-27 判、09-28 实测换理由、**09-28 第三轮裁决后落地 = -54** |

| 括号粘贴的 **DLL 侧标记合成** | ⛔ 只拒这一半 | 标记必须先于粘贴字节入流，而粘贴字节由 conhost 整串注入；DLL 读 CONIN$ 会与应用 pump 抢队列（偷键盘事件），peek-then-inject 没有时序保证。另外四处义务 #83 已做完（存位/应答/复位/-36 起跨重建携带） | #83 | 09-27 |
| 延迟换行 | ⛔ 不做 | 显示面就是 conhost 本身；DECAWM `?7` 已由 #61 建模（-22，I35），改成立即换行只会与 conhost 分叉；caps 的 `smam`/`rmam` 同判不进条目 | `—` 行 #61 | 09-26，09-27 补 |
| 图形协议（Sixel / kitty / iTerm2） | ⛔ 不做，**理由已换成对的** | 旧句"物理显示面画不出"不再是要害：#104（-48）在走 render 时借掉宿主的 `ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_PROCESSING`，#103（-49）把输出收成"只发真正变化的格子" ⇒ DCS 载荷既没有解析者也没有承载面。**#52 那句"不声明 sixel 能力"本轮被实测重新定性（#107）**：`;4` 不是本库编的，是 MSFT `DeviceAttributes` 的原话（`adaptDispatch.cpp:1436-1461`，`:1441` "4 = Sixel Graphics"），而那份克隆里**真有 `SixelParser.cpp`**（GDI 侧 `renderer/gdi/state.cpp:126` 为它设位图拉伸）⇒ 宿主报 `;4` 是真能力，本库照抄是 deviation 8 的"同一会话不得两个身份"，**撤掉它才是撒谎**；而**现网 Java 链（`lib/JLine3.jar`，746 class 常量池扫描 + jline4 对照臂）没有任何 sixel 消费者**，所以今天没有程序会把它读成能力——jline4 那条 `isModeSupported=false` 覆写是**换树出货那天**的挡板，不是今天的。载荷在本库的命运**已从注释升级成证人**：DCS 六色体一律整段丢弃 + 一票 `RC_UN_DCS`，跨 chunk 分段不投票（可续状态），host 7279/0、live 6501/0，红臂 `dcs-leak`/`dcs-silent` 各自归位。**唯一诚实的替代做法**（把载荷转交给宿主、让 conhost 自己画）与 #104/#103 正面冲突 ⇒ ③ 类，代价=放弃 VTP 借还或放弃格子差分，不开单 | `—` 行 #52 #103 #104 #107 | 09-28 复核、**09-28 实测换定性** |
| **先说清楚：下划线是画的。** 这一行只管另外四样：斜体 / 删除线 / 下划线**颜色** / 闪烁 | ⛔ 这四样的**绘制**不做（前三样存状态，闪烁连状态都不存） | **拆行的动机**：旧写法把"下划线色不画"和"下划线不画"混在一格，下一个读的人会去补一个已经存在的行为。**画的这一半**（本轮打开确认）：`Render.cpp:152` `if (s->underline) n \|= RC_LVB_UNDERSCORE;`，紧挨着 `:153` 的 `if (s->inverse) n |= RC_LVB_REVERSE;` —— 下划线与反色**有承载位并且真的进 `CHAR_INFO`**，细节见 `ANSI_SUPPORTS.md` §2.7。**不画的四样，各自的锚点与各自的形状**：① 斜体 `Render.cpp:979`/`:980`（`case 3: s->italic = 1;` / `case 23: s->italic = 0;`）**存了**，但本轮 grep `->italic` 在整个 `src/c/conemu` 里**只命中这两行** ⇒ 状态没有任何读者；② 删除线 `:1003`/`:1004`（`s->crossed`）同样存而不绘，同样零读者；③ 下划线**颜色** `:1015` `case 58:` —— 参数被完整跳过、随后 `unsupported(g, RC_UN_COLON)`，是"读懂了但拒绝"，不是没解析；④ 闪烁 `:1000` `case 5: case 6: case 25: break;` **连状态都不存**（注释点名上游 `Ansi.cpp:3530` 也没有状态）。**承载面**：`conattrs.hpp:9` 的 `META_ATTRS` 全集本轮逐字读过，只有 lead/trail 字节、三条 grid line、reverse、underscore —— 没有斜体/删除线/闪烁/下划线色的位；看着像空位的 0x0400/0x0800/0x1000 会被 conhost **真画成网格线**、0x2000 被掩掉 ⇒ 借位去画等于画错东西。**归属**：①② 属 ① 类（没有承载面），③ 属 ①+②（既没有位可放、把默认色说成"已按请求设置"又是撒谎），④ 属 ①（上游与本库都没有状态） | `—` 行 #94 #110 | 09-27 判、**09-28 拆清"画/不画"** |
| OSC 9 的**危险**子命令（`9;1` sleep、`9;2` MessageBox、`9;3` 改 ConEmu 页签文字、`9;5` 等键并写环境变量、`9;6` GuiMacro、`9;7` DoProcess、`9;8` **把环境变量的值回送进流**、`9;10` 切 xterm 仿真、`9;11` 注释） | ⛔ 永不执行 | RCE 安全地板（I21）。**安全子集已经做了**：#63（= WORK_ORDER T7，-21）按 MSFT `DoConEmuAction` 的边界实现 `9;4`/`9;9`/`9;12`，其余一律送 `UnknownSequence()`（`adaptDispatch.cpp:3558-3641`，`else` 那一支就是它，"一律拒绝"是那份文件的**结构**而不是缺代码）。**#111 把这句从"我们没写所以不执行"升级成两件可红的证**：① 那一族 **1..12 全数逐条对表**，落在 `ANSI_SUPPORTS.md` §2.8.1（来源本轮自己打开：ConEmu 官方页 `cache/p111/conemu-ansi.html`、MSFT 那份 `DoConEmuAction`、本库 `Render.cpp::osc9_action`）；② **能力地板**做进 build 门禁——进程/对话框/COM/环境写/远程内存那一族入口点在四个 dll 的导入表里都不存在（`build.sh::gate_dll` 的 `noexec` 段，日志句："no process/dialog/env-write entrypoint imported (OSC 9 floor holds) OK"），所以"执行某条子命令"这件事**不是没写，是链不上**；地板之外也明写：`LoadLibrary*`/`GetProcAddress`/`Sleep` 确实在导入表里（#88/#104 的运行期探测与静态 CRT），它们与 `osc9_action` 无关由**行为腿**负责。腿：host `gm_osc9` 的 0..31 完整扫描（拒绝的号一个状态都不许改）＋ live `caseOsc9Family`（`9;1;5000` 实测 0 ms、`9;8` 回读 CONIN$ 为空、窗口标题不变）；红臂 `act-9-5`/`leak-9-8`/`osc9-silent`/`osc9-cwd` 四档各自点名。**旧条目那句"安全子集已立项 T7"已失效**（T7 早落地） | #63 #111 | 09-28 复核、**09-28 补成可红证据** |
| OSC 8 超链接 | ⛔ 不做 | 属主 2026-09-26 定案（原话"osc 8不做"）。技术账不变：链接是**区间**不是 cell，只能住在 `rowWrap[]` 旁边并继承 I20 那条"读不回来"的债 | #56 | 09-26 |
| `?3`（132 列） | ⛔ 不做（拒绝成立，**但原来的机制句是反的**；DECRQM 从沉默改成答 4） | 实测否掉了翻案前提，也否掉了旧理由的因果（`cache/w109/report-classic.txt`、`report-wt4.txt`、`rect-wt.txt`，2026-09-28）：**旧句"改列数只能靠 `SetConsoleScreenBufferSize`，那会移动用户自己的控制台窗口"两头都错**——A `SetConsoleScreenBufferSize(X=2040)` rc=True 而 VISIBLE 与 hwndrect（633x416 px）**纹丝不动**，它改的是回滚宽度，不动窗口也承载不了可见宽；真正能改可见宽的是 B `SetConsoleWindowInfo`，它 rc=True 把 VISIBLE 推到 140 的同时**把用户窗口从 633x416 px 加宽到 873x416 px**（左上角不动）。⇒ 结论仍是"没有不伤宿主的承载面"，但成立的是 ③ 类（与 #101/#103 的"几何由宿主定、本库只跟随"冲突，代价=应用要 132 列时拿不到），不是"某个 API 会动窗口"这一句。ConPTY/WT 侧另有一层：B 返回 TRUE 且 shadow 读到 140 列，而**第二进程**测到真实 WT 窗口 8 次采样恒为 `(200,208)-(1209,723) 1009x515 px` ⇒ 这条路只会让应用**读到假宽度**。第三条 `ResizePseudoConsole` 本机导出但要用 `CreatePseudoConsole` 给创建者的 HPCON（`winconpty.cpp:509`；神谕里也只有创建者调：`ConptyConnection.cpp:442/610/618`），控制台内进程拿不到。三神谕：MSFT 要 `?40h` 门开才做且真调 `_api.ResizeWindow`（`adaptDispatch.cpp:1715-1731`）；ghostty 门关时连位都清（`Terminal.zig:3958-3965`）、门开时只改自己的 grid（:3971-3977）；wezterm 不做。**应答口径**：`DECRQM ?3$p` 现答 **4 = permanently reset**（`DispatchTypes.hpp:558`；jline4 读 4 得 NOT_SUPPORTED，`AbstractTerminal.java:685-689`），因为派单原来写的"应答 1"按 #93 词表等于宣称现在就是 132 列（I26 假话），答 2 又等于宣称"可以被设置"（jline4 文档 :665 原话）也是假话；set 侧一字未动，`?3h/?3l` 仍投 `RC_UN_MODE` | `—` 行、#109（含三档突变归位：`dcm-answer2` host 红 3、`dcm-silent` host 红 5、`dcm-wire` **host 全盲 / live 红**） | 09-26 判、**09-28 实测改口径并落地 -52** |
| `u8`/`u9` 进 caps | ⛔ 不声明（判决不变，**理由已换对且第三条已实测**） | 三条真理由：`u8` 是含 `%[` 的**模式**而本栈 DA1 是定值 36 字节 ⇒ "声明串==实发串"永不成立（②类）；`u9` 是主 DA **请求**不是 DA2 应答 ⇒ 填进去对同一会话另一个解析器撒谎（②类）；声明会崩自家 `Curses.tputs`（`terminal/src/main/java/org/jline/utils/Curses.java:425-426` 的 `if ("cdoxXs".indexOf(ch) < 0) throw`，③类：与现有 reader 冲突。⚠ 同仓另有一个 `curses/…/Curses.java`，只写文件名会让人开错）。**旧四条里那条"没有读者"已删，而且是实测删的**：WSL ncurses 6.3.20211021 `tput -T xterm-256color u8` 正常吐出 `^[[?%[;0123456789]c`（exit 0），`u9` 吐出 `^[[c` —— 非 jline 的读者不但存在，还照原样把**模式**发出去（长名 `tput user8` 反倒不认，读者用短名）。⇒ 判决不动，动的是理由的诚实性。细节与两处订正（`infocmp` 行号 200/201、DA1 漏 `;21`）在 #86 行 | #86 / T-10 #115 | 09-28 改判、**09-28 实测补全** |
| `?1048`、`?12`、`?2027` 的 DECRQM 应答 | ~~⛔ 沉默并计数~~ → **✅ 答 0**（G11，build -54）。**模式本身仍然不做**，那一半的理由留着：1048 是事件不是可报告的状态、12（光标闪烁）无承载面、2027 是 grapheme-cluster reflow 而本库按格折行没有簇状态 | **沉默不是"诚实"，是三神谕都出声而本库不出声**（本轮逐条打开）：MSFT `RequestMode` 先 `state = DECRPM_Unsupported` 再 switch，任何它没点名编号一律带数字回答（`src/terminal/adapter/adaptDispatch.cpp:1939`，词表 `DispatchTypes.hpp:552-559`）；ghostty `getReport` 走 `modeFromInt(...) orelse .not_recognized`（`src/terminal/modes.zig:80-102`）；wezterm 对 `DecPrivateMode::Unspecified` 置 `recognized = false` **照样回**（`term/src/terminalstate/mod.rs:1520-1526`）。⇒ 落点在 `Render.cpp` 的 `p` 臂：`if (!mode_status(g, g->args[0], &st)) st = 0;`（`:2197`），注释点名 GAP G11（`:2188`）。**0 只声称"这个模型不持有这个状态"，是此处唯一一定为真的话**。两个**永久**数字不退这一步：`?3` 答 4（#109，实测撑腰）、`?2` 答 3（#114/DECANM），它们是挣来的，别人要进这个公司得拿出同等级的事实。**腿改名**：`geo_decrpm_unmodelled`（`RenderCheck.cpp:4107`，`UNMODELLED[]` 逐个断"answer=0 且不再投 mode 票"），旧的 `geo_decrpm_silence` 只剩 `:4203` 那格 `grid_ok` 的标签文字——标签与函数名已经不同代，引腿名以函数名为准。**set 侧一字未动**：`?2027h` 仍投 `RC_UN_MODE`（`RenderCheck.cpp:4161-4162` 是这一条的腿） | #87 G11 | 09-27 判沉默、**09-28 第三轮裁决后改答 0** |

**2026-09-28 这次复核对 §5 动了什么**（旧表逐字在附录 A，被推翻的说法不在本节保留）：

- **删掉一行**："DECSCUSR 条形 5/6 不做"。它已被 #88 落地推翻（-38 planner+seam、-39 补 `STAT_SHAPE` 与 live 腿），
  而且工单当时的期待本身被实测否定——运行时探测照做了，本机 `kernel32.dll` 的 1697 个具名导出里没有 `SetConsoleCursorShape`，
  所以 live 两架构跑的就是 absent 臂。**留在"不做"清单里会让下一个人重开它。**
- **半行失效**：OSC 9 那行的"安全子集已立项 T7"。T7 已落地 = #63（-21），此行现在只保留危险半边不做。
- **两条理由换对**：图形协议（旧句"物理显示面画不出"→ #104 借掉宿主解析器 + #103 只发差分）；
  鼠标族（旧句"模式位无从生效"在 #81/#95 之后已不成立；第二轮补的那句"现在站得住的只有不写报告、不声明 `kmous`"
  **也被第三轮裁决推掉了**——报告编码器与共用注入线已在 build -54 落地，本节能留下的只有"自主投递"那一半，
  而它的理由已点名到 `ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_INPUT` 的具体代价与编号）
- **补进五条**：`?3`、`u8`/`u9`、DECCKM/kitty、`?1048`/`?12`/`?2027` 的应答集（**09-28 第三轮起这一格不再是"沉默集"，
  是"答 0 集"**——模式仍未做，但问题有答案了）、闪烁并入斜体那行。
  它们此前只散在 §3 各自的行里，而"防重开"的清单必须一次看全 —— 这也是本节存在的唯一理由。
- **反向核对**：本轮把 §3 里所有 `状态=⛔/◐` 与含"不做/沉默/declined"的行扫了一遍，没有发现第 6 条已落地却仍挂在
  不做清单上的条目；但 #52 是反例性质的提醒——它的 `状态` 被抽成 `🔲 未做`，实际"该做的覆写"在依赖层已写、
  只是没随 jline4 树发布 ⇒ 这一行**不是**"不做"，是"做了但未出货"，本节只在图形协议那行引用它，不把它当拒绝。
  **#107 把这句再推一步**：未出货的那层**今天也没有可挡的东西**——现网 `lib/JLine3.jar` 的 746 个 class 常量池里
  `sixel`/`isModeSupported` **0 命中**（对照臂在 jline4 源码命中 4 个文件，所以 0 是"没有"不是"搜不到"），
  链条身份由 `lib/bootstrap.lua:71→:206` 逐字确定，不是按 jar 名猜的。⇒ 那条覆写是**换树出货那天的挡板**；
  今天真正生效的义务只有"载荷整段丢弃 + 计 `RC_UN_DCS`"，而它现在有腿了（host 7279/0、live 6501/0，两档红臂归位）。

## 7 证人缺口：这张表自己承认的弱证（复核时优先级最高）

下面这段是原 §12 表后那段"the honest gaps"的逐字保留 —— 它不参与合并，因为它是**关于整张表的元结论**，
不属于任何一个编号。复核时先读它，再决定重跑哪几条腿。

**#80 leaves one half of a claim deliberately un-witnessed**: DECOM is carried across a rebuild by `build_model` so that `?6$p` keeps answering, but the DECSTBM region is *not* carried (that is `Render.h`'s own DECSTBM note, and `live34-x64e.txt` measured it), and without a region the origin bit has no observable effect on a screen -- same base, same clamp. The live leg therefore asserts the band and says in its comment that the mode bit is kept for the answer and not for the picture. A future reader should not "fix" that by inventing a gate that cannot fail.

The honest gaps in the table, stated rather than papered over. **Rows #13 and #14 are dbcli's pager and have no
`render.dll` gate at any level** -- their only witness is a live console leg, which is weaker than the rest of
this section and is the first thing to re-run if the pager misbehaves after a jline change; #14's leg is at least
doubled by a `javap` reading of the shipped bytecode, #13's is not. **Row #13's `ceon` leg is unreadable as
witnessed** (the harness's own prompt regex cannot parse a grid that still holds 83 literal escapes), so the row
rests on one leg, not two. **Row #16's ruling now has a produced A/B, and it is the wrong A/B**: what
`wide1516-20260925-13.txt` measures is *line width* across two geometries, not a wide-buffer scroll, so §9's
scroll leg is still owed and deviation 1 still rests on the planner's algebra. And **row #23's legs and the -6
file they replace were run against different jars** (`2297d517…` vs `0ea9b33c…`), so the two agree on the shape
of the status rows and nothing about Java-side behaviour. Three more, from the 2026-09-25 rows: **#37 and #38
have no real-ConEmu-window leg** -- `NativeWinSysTerminal` also reports `windows-conemu` when `ConEmuPID` is
set, so the third host in this family is unmeasured and only inferred from the two that were. **#39's snap leg
proves the call is safe, not that it helps** -- **overturned 2026-09-27 by reading the leg that already ships.**
`Render.java:3475 caseSnapOnInput` scrolls the window to the buffer top, asserts the prompt row is *below* the
viewport, calls `snap()`, then reads the window back through `consoleView()` and asserts `v[0] + WIN_H - 1 == want`
(the least slide that reveals the cursor's row) and `v[6] == want`, plus "the snap wrote none of the N scrollback
rows", no cell painted, no re-adopt, no flush, `snaps == snaps0 + 1`. Both arches print it green at -36
(`cache/audit90/live-gate.txt`). The "useful" half was in the standing gate all along and this sentence was stale. And **#40's witness is a probe harness,
not a gate** -- the fix is in dbcli's `Console.java`, so neither standing `render.dll` gate can carry it. The
same applies to the CJK-doubling re-check that followed it (`cache/wt-doubling-ab-20260925`): a real Windows
Terminal window read `0 doubled pair(s)` with 14 filler rows intact at `frames_read=192`, and the classic
conhost leg where the renderer *is* engaged read the same `0 doubled pair(s)` at `frames_read=134`, but only
the shipped arm was taken through that harness -- the pre-fix arm rests on #37's ENGAGED/off matrix, not on a
same-harness before/after. **Seven more, from the rows added since:** **#48 has a unit red/green pair and no
post-fix live arm** -- the symptom was watched in a real window and reproduced there, but what closed it is
`StatusTest`'s six cases (`tests=6 found=6 succeeded=6 failed=0`) plus a single-block revert as the control
(`succeeded=4 failed=2`, and the two red are exactly the bordered-resize cases); a library-side proof for a
symptom that only ever shows in a window. **#43 is stated as mechanism-removed, not symptom-witnessed**, for the
reason given below. **#44's proof depends on a hook that ships**: `Java_Render_faultRect` is in `build.sh`'s
export whitelist because the gate links against the released DLL, so `render.dll` exports a fault injector that
nothing at runtime calls -- said here rather than buried, because it is the kind of seam a later reader otherwise
assumes is dead weight. **#45 is a gap that is not about evidence** -- #38's classification fix is still not in
`lib/`, and until it is, no ConEmu-window leg can be taken at all. **(Both halves of that sentence are false as of
2026-09-27: `lib/dbcli.jar` carries `org/dbcli/WinSysTerminal.class` at 17087 bytes with `pseudoConsole`,
`ANSICON_DEF`, `WT_SESSION`, `windows-conemu` and `snapOnInput` in its constant pool, which is exactly what #38
ruled should ship. So the blocker is gone and the only remaining half of #45 is the one that was never measured:
no ConEmu-window classification leg exists.)** **#53's own list is still open at one item**,
and #51 blocks on it: the interim marker has a single slot where DECRQM/DECRPM need a set. And **#47/#48 leave
their library half un-rehearsed** -- `JLINE_CHANGES.md`'s "What is not witnessed" is the list (`reset()` has no
red/green pair, `resize(Size)` has no unit case of its own), because those are edits to someone else's codebase
and this file does not get to claim their coverage. If a row's witness file is missing, the row is
unproven, whatever the two standing gates say.

**Row #41's two claims are both answered, one by invalidating a witness and one by removing a mechanism.** The
20-column staircase was never on the screen. #42 shows it to be the probe's `ReadConsoleOutputW` readback of
conhost's *shadow* buffer, which keeps the pre-resize pitch while the window itself renders clean -- one fixture
run through two resize paths, with `uia-resize.ps1` as the only qualified resize witness and `pw-shot.ps1`
(`PrintWindow`) as the witness to what a user sees. The trigger is named and it is the harness, and the rule that
survives the finding is in `.dsh/memory/reference/reference-wt-geometry-witness.md`: on a `--host wt` leg a
readback is not evidence about geometry. The second claim -- close the bar, *then* resize, and the screen wipes to
one prompt row -- is #43, where the mechanism was a scroll region a torn-down bar left behind and the removal is
`Status.reset()` writing `CSI r` with no parameters. That row is deliberately narrower than the old paragraph:
`clearScrollRegion()`, the call #41 said `More.init()` was missing, **does not exist anywhere in the tree**, and
`More` reaches the teardown through `close()`, which is `hide()` + `reset()`. But **the leg has not been re-taken
since the fix landed** -- the one measurement on record predates the `Status.java` that carries it and ran through
the resize path #42 voided -- so #43 closes as "mechanism removed", not as "symptom witnessed gone". The script
that would take it is ready at `cache/resize43-20260925/leg43.json`.
